**[00:14:57.130] Presentation video** (asr-000229)  
may tinatawag na doorway option (cross-track) na puwede

**[00:14:59.130] Presentation video** (asr-000230)  
kaming kumuha ng isang elective subject

**[00:15:00.960] Presentation video** (asr-000231)  
kung gusto namin.

**[00:15:02.280] Presentation video** (asr-000232)  
Maganda siya para sa akin kasi nakakatulong po ako sa pamilya ko.

**[00:15:05.780] Presentation video** (asr-000233)  
And I'm currently now a make-up artist po.

**[00:15:07.880] Presentation video** (asr-000234)  
Pero walang batang umaabot doon nang mag-isa.

**[00:15:11.980] Presentation video** (asr-000235)  
Kailangan niya ng gurong may sapat na oras at suporta.

**[00:15:16.020] Presentation video** (asr-000236)  
Dati, full-load,

**[00:15:19.700] Presentation video** (asr-000237)  
madaming gawain, dismissal na pero kailangan ko pa ring tapusin.

**[00:15:22.220] Presentation video** (asr-000238)  
Pero ngayon, naging mas magaan.

**[00:15:24.360] Presentation video** (asr-000239)  
Mas naka-focus na ako sa aking mga tungkulin bilang guro sa mga bata.

**[00:15:27.720] Presentation video** (asr-000240)  
Sa wakas po, mapapagaan po nito ang aming trabaho.

**[00:15:32.680] Presentation video** (asr-000241)  
At makakapag-focus po kami sa pagtuturo namin.

**[00:15:38.020] Presentation video** (asr-000242)  
Siyempre, nandiyan yung number one bond paper,

**[00:15:40.620] Presentation video** (asr-000243)  
ink for printer namin.

**[00:15:42.320] Presentation video** (asr-000244)  
Kahit paisa-isang bilihan mo,

**[00:15:44.000] Presentation video** (asr-000245)  
pero kapag binilang mo at pinagsama-sama,

**[00:15:46.420] Presentation video** (asr-000246)  
marami rin siya at malaki talaga.

**[00:15:48.820] Presentation video** (asr-000247)  
We are grateful na nadagdagan yung teaching allowance namin

**[00:15:51.600] Presentation video** (asr-000248)  
kasi malaking tulong para sa gayon,

**[00:15:54.840] Presentation video** (asr-000249)  
mas maturuan at maibigay namin yung pangangailangan ng mga bata.

**[00:15:59.060] Presentation video** (asr-000250)  
At hindi lang trabaho ang kailangang gumaan.

**[00:16:02.600] Presentation video** (asr-000251)  
Kailangan ding magkaroon ng pagkakataong umunlad ang guro.

**[00:16:07.040] Presentation video** (asr-000252)  
Ngayon ay nakikita na rin namin ang aming landas

**[00:16:10.500] Presentation video** (asr-000253)  
para sa aming growth or pag-unlad,

**[00:16:13.860] Presentation video** (asr-000254)  
lalong-lalo na sa aming pagtanda.

**[00:16:16.440] Presentation video** (asr-000255)  
There are times na nakakalimutan na rin namin yung sarili naming well-being,

**[00:16:19.240] Presentation video** (asr-000256)  
sarili naming health.

**[00:16:20.680] Presentation video** (asr-000257)  
With this medical allowance,

**[00:16:23.360] Presentation video** (asr-000258)  
nagkaroon kami ng pakiramdam na the government is also looking

**[00:16:28.020] Presentation video** (asr-000259)  
at our physical well-being.

**[00:16:30.440] Presentation video** (asr-000260)  
At sa paaralan,

**[00:16:31.800] Presentation video** (asr-000261)  
ang pag-aalaga ay hindi nagtatapos sa pagtuturo.

**[00:16:36.100] Presentation video** (asr-000262)  
Sa hirap talaga ng buhay ngayon,

**[00:16:38.960] Presentation video** (asr-000263)  
talagang masasabi kong napakalaking tulong

**[00:16:41.980] Presentation video** (asr-000264)  
para sa aming mga magulang yung feeding program na ito.

**[00:16:45.700] Presentation video** (asr-000265)  
Minsan, may mga bagay na hindi mo kayang sabihin sa parents and friends mo,

**[00:16:50.060] Presentation video** (asr-000266)  
at nakakagaan po ng loob

**[00:16:52.140] Presentation video** (asr-000267)  
kapag may taong nakikinig at may concern sa iyo.

**[00:16:56.460] Presentation video** (asr-000268)  
Bilang magulang,

**[00:16:57.720] Presentation video** (asr-000269)  
kampante kami dahil hindi lang natututukan

**[00:17:02.500] Presentation video** (asr-000270)  
ang kanilang pag-aaral,

**[00:17:04.100] Presentation video** (asr-000271)  
kundi ang kanilang kaligtasan.

**[00:17:07.000] Presentation video** (asr-000272)  
Kahit may sakuna,

**[00:17:08.660] Presentation video** (asr-000273)  
kaming mga guro at mga bata ay alam na po yung mga paghahanda at dapat gawin.

**[00:17:13.960] Presentation video** (asr-000274)  
Sa katunayan, may mga safety measures na po kami, may mga materials po kami.

**[00:17:16.540] Presentation video** (asr-000275)  
At para magawa ito,

**[00:17:18.760] Presentation video** (asr-000276)  
dapat maihatid ang mga pangangailangan ng bawat paaralan.

**[00:17:23.180] Presentation video** (asr-000277)  
Maganda yung epekto,

**[00:17:24.640] Presentation video** (asr-000278)  
mabilis at maayos yung dating ng mga learning

**[00:17:27.560] Presentation video** (asr-000279)  
materials sa school.

**[00:17:29.560] Presentation video** (asr-000280)  
Kasi nagiging masaya yung mga estudyante,

**[00:17:31.560] Presentation video** (asr-000281)  
yung mga teachers.

**[00:17:36.030] Presentation video** (asr-000282)  
Ang daming tumutulong sa ating paaralan,

**[00:17:38.030] Presentation video** (asr-000283)  
yung ating mga stakeholders na laging nandiyan

**[00:17:40.030] Presentation video** (asr-000284)  
para tumulong sa mga needs ng school.

**[00:17:42.030] Presentation video** (asr-000285)  
Pero mas napapadali ang pagtulong kapag sentralisado

**[00:17:47.030] Presentation video** (asr-000286)  
at malalaman na mismo kung ano ang pangangailangan ng paaralan.

**[00:17:51.030] Presentation video** (asr-000287)  
Mas panatag po kami ngayon dahil ramdam namin

**[00:17:54.030] Presentation video** (asr-000288)  
na ang bawat piso ay nagagamit nang tama

**[00:17:57.030] Presentation video** (asr-000289)  
at napupunta talaga sa edukasyon ng mga bata.

**[00:18:01.030] Presentation video** (asr-000290)  
May mga nasimulan na. At mas marami pa tayong gagawin.

**[00:18:06.030] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000291)  
Edukasyon pa rin ang siyang pangunahing nagtataguyod ng kakayahan.

**[00:18:11.030] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-000292)  
Kung kaya, ito ang nangunguna sa listahan ng administrasyong ito.

**[00:18:17.030] Presentation video** (asr-000293)  
Kaya malinaw ang susunod na hakbang:

**[00:18:20.030] Presentation video** (asr-000294)  
ituloy ang nasimulan at palawakin ang naaabot.

**[00:18:37.800] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000295)  
Thank you very much Madam Chair for the opportunity.

**[00:18:40.800] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000296)  
For fiscal year 2027, the proposed national expenditure program or NEP budget

**[00:18:45.800] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000297)  
for the DepEd is P975.96 billion including funding for attached agencies

**[00:18:51.800] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000298)  
namely the National Book Development Board, NBDB, P169.9 million,

**[00:18:56.800] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000299)  
National Council for Children's Television, P76 million,

**[00:18:59.800] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000300)  
National Museum of the Philippines, P1.56 billion,

**[00:19:02.800] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000301)  
Philippine High School for the Arts, P135.3 million,

**[00:19:05.800] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000302)  
Teacher Education Council, P360.3 million,

**[00:19:08.800] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000303)  
and the National Academy of Sports, P258.5 million.

**[00:19:20.810] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000304)  
This slide presents the comparative budget for the 2026 NEP, 2026 GAA, and 2027 NEP, Madam Chair.

**[00:19:32.810] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000305)  
The 2027 NEP level budget amounts to P975.96 billion as mentioned earlier

**[00:19:39.810] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000306)  
which is P39.35 billion lower

**[00:19:41.810] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000307)  
than the 2026 GAA.

**[00:19:44.810] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000308)  
Karamihan po sa ating attached agencies ay nakapagtala ng lower budget levels

**[00:19:52.810] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000309)  
for fiscal year 2027 compared to the fiscal year 2026 GAA

**[00:19:56.810] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000310)  
except for the National Museum of the Philippines which received a higher proposed allocation.

**[00:20:01.810] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000311)  
Next slide please.

**[00:20:05.370] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000312)  
This slide, Madam Chair, Your Honors, presents the comparison of the 2026 NEP versus GAA versus 2027 NEP.

**[00:20:13.370] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000313)  
For DepEd proper alone.

**[00:20:15.370] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000314)  
So, personal services continues to go up and represents about 79% of the total budget

**[00:20:23.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000315)  
at around P766 billion approximately.

**[00:20:26.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000316)  
MOOE and capital outlay both decreased compared to the 2026 GAA.

**[00:20:32.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000317)  
MOOE declined by P33 billion while capital outlay records a large decrease of P67 billion.

**[00:20:39.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000318)  
Ito po yung overall picture.

**[00:20:44.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000319)  
Next slide please.

**[00:20:47.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000320)  
This slide presents the budget utilization rate of DepEd which obligation stands at 51.54%

**[00:20:55.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000321)  
as of August 20, 2026 and disbursement rate of 89.28%, Your Honor.

**[00:21:02.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000322)  
Next slide please.

**[00:21:04.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000323)  
This slide Madam Chair, Your Honors, presents some of the highlights of the 2027 NEP.

**[00:21:10.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000324)  
Starting with personnel improvements.

**[00:21:12.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000325)  
So, we start with the expanded career progression where we target to promote more than 135,000 teachers and school heads

**[00:21:19.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000326)  
with a corresponding allocation of 12.09 billion.

**[00:21:23.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000327)  
Naglaan din po tayo ng 3.75 billion para sa training ng mahigit 500,000 teachers and laptops for 50,000 teachers, Your Honor.

**[00:21:33.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000328)  
For the learning environment, we have 17.31 billion to put up over 4,000 classrooms.

**[00:21:41.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000329)  
Sa aspeto naman po ng digital transformation, bukod sa nabanggit na laptops,

**[00:21:47.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000330)  
meron din pong 3.37 billion para sa internet renewal of over 14,000 schools, ICT solutions and capacity building.

**[00:21:56.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000331)  
We also have allocated 6.4 billion for the production and procurement of textbooks and workbooks and other learning materials.

**[00:22:03.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000332)  
And for inclusive education, we have 2.63 billion to support special needs education,

**[00:22:10.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000333)  
indigenous peoples education, madrasa education, multigrade education, alternative modes and alternative learning systems or ALS.

**[00:22:20.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000334)  
For the feeding program, 11.43 billion was allocated to support 3.69 million learners.

**[00:22:25.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000335)  
We're also asking additional for inclusive education as well as governance and innovation.

**[00:22:31.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000336)  
And of course, our attached agencies also have their respective requests, Your Honor.

**[00:22:35.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000337)  
The National Book Development Board is requesting an additional 186 million.

**[00:22:40.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000338)  
For book fairs, readership promotion, policy development, research and advocacy,

**[00:22:44.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000339)  
the National Council for Children's Television is requesting an additional 61 million

**[00:22:49.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000340)  
for child-friendly, educational and values-oriented TV content, research and ICT support.

**[00:22:54.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000341)  
The National Museum of the Philippines is requesting an additional 255 million

**[00:22:59.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000342)  
for digital transformation, public access, museum engagement and component museums nationwide.

**[00:23:05.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000343)  
The Philippine High School for the Arts is requesting an additional 161 million

**[00:23:10.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000344)  
for campus rehabilitation, facilities and equipment, student support and quality arts education.

**[00:23:15.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000345)  
The National Academy of Sports is requesting an additional 580 million

**[00:23:19.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000346)  
for sports development, competitions, student-athlete support, curriculum and facilities.

**[00:23:24.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000347)  
And the Teacher Education Council is requesting an additional 498 million for salaries,

**[00:23:29.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000348)  
teacher education and graduate scholarships, and regional hubs.

**[00:23:33.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000349)  
So, ito po yung some of the salient points

**[00:23:39.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000350)  
of the 27 budget, Your Honor, and the resource persons, including myself,

**[00:23:45.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000351)  
are most willing to answer any questions from the distinguished members of the committee.

**[00:23:50.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000352)  
Madam Chair, thank you very much.

**[00:23:52.000] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-000353)  
Thank you very much, Secretary Angara.

**[00:24:01.820] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-000354)  
Before we proceed to the interpellation and discussion,

**[00:24:04.820] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-000355)  
the Chair would like to acknowledge the presence of the following members.

**[00:24:11.820] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-000356)  
Deputy Minority Leader Antonio Tinio,

**[00:24:15.820] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-000357)  
Representative Attorney Karen Lagbas,

**[00:24:18.820] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-000358)  
Deputy Majority Leader Mica Gonzales,

**[00:24:21.820] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-000359)  
Vice Chairperson Rommel Angara,

**[00:24:24.820] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-000360)  
Vice Chairperson Rufus Rodriguez,

**[00:24:27.820] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-000361)  
Good morning, Madam Chair, and best wishes to our favorite department. Thank you.

**[00:24:33.820] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-000362)  
Representative Milagros Magsaysay,

**[00:24:37.820] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-000363)  
Deputy Speaker Janet Garin,

**[00:24:40.820] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-000364)  
Representative Renee Co.

**[00:24:45.320] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-000365)  
May I remind our distinguished colleagues

**[00:24:48.320] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-000366)  
that each member is allotted five minutes for questions or interpellation,

**[00:24:53.320] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-000367)  
excluding the responses of the resource persons.

**[00:24:56.320] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-000368)  
When the bell sounds, the member has 30 seconds to conclude.

**[00:24:59.320] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-000369)  
Consistent with the long-standing practice of the House,

**[00:25:03.320] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-000370)  
the Chair shall recognize minority, independent, and majority members

**[00:25:08.320] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-000371)  
in an alternating manner when practicable.

**[00:25:12.320] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-000372)  
The Chair first now recognizes,

**[00:25:15.320] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-000373)  
the member of the minority.

**[00:25:17.320] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-000374)  
So allow me to recognize Representative Perci Cendaña

**[00:25:21.320] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-000375)  
for the first question.

**[00:25:23.320] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000376)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Chair,

**[00:25:26.320] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000377)  
at magandang umaga po sa ating lahat.

**[00:25:28.320] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000378)  
Bago po magsimula, baka pwede po muna itong bilangin sa aking five minutes,

**[00:25:33.320] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000379)  
gusto ko lamang pong batiin lahat ng mga guro na narito na Happy Teacher's Month po sa inyo.

**[00:25:39.320] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000380)  
Para pong mahina yung palakpak for our teachers,

**[00:25:44.210] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000381)  
pwede na pong magsimula yung five minutes, Madam Chair.

**[00:25:50.510] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000382)  
Sige po.

**[00:25:53.510] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000383)  
This is a very pressing matter because of what has been happening in our schools the past few months.

**[00:26:01.510] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000384)  
Madam Chair, nakababahala po na mismo ang EDCOM 2 nakita na ang Pilipinas na ngayon ang bullying capital of the world.

**[00:26:12.510] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000385)  
Inuulit ko po, hindi ito magandang klase ng gold medal.

**[00:26:16.510] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000386)  
Ito yung gold medal na dapat hindi natin sinusuot with pride.

**[00:26:19.510] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000387)  
Again, the Philippines is the bullying capital of the world.

**[00:26:23.510] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000388)  
This is saddening as it is alarming dahil alam nating bumababa ang learning competencies ng ating mga studyante kapag nabubully sila.

**[00:26:33.510] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000389)  
Bullying is also among the reasons pointed out why the recent cases of school violence happened and why it is alarmingly increasing.

**[00:26:43.510] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000390)  
Kaya nga po, kahit ako hindi nakaligtas sa bullying,

**[00:26:48.510] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000391)  
from no less than a school principal, shout out po sa principal ang Dimasalang High School, National High School po sa Masbate.

**[00:26:56.510] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000392)  
Madam Chair, we'd like to ask the DepEd, ano po ang plano ng DepEd para mapigilan ang bullying at karahasan within our schools?

**[00:27:06.510] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000393)  
We have a lot of programs and we're looking at, magandang umaga, Perci.

**[00:27:14.400] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000394)  
We're looking at full implementation of the anti-bullying law, revised anti-bullying law.

**[00:27:18.400] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000395)  
And in the last year, we've seen a, dumami na po talaga yung functioning child protection committees which is a requirement of the anti-bullying law.

**[00:27:28.400] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000396)  
I think if I'm not mistaken, we have over 30,000, there are over 36,000 of our schools already have functioning child protection committees.

**[00:27:38.400] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000397)  
And ang ginawa po namin dahil nakita po ng EDCOM nga na iilan lang po ang dati-dati-rati, about a year and a half ago, ang may functioning child protection committees.

**[00:27:46.400] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000398)  
So, that's one. And the second is under the new mental health and well-being law, we're ramping up the hiring.

**[00:27:56.400] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000399)  
In fact, we shortened the time for the hiring of the school council associates.

**[00:28:00.400] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000400)  
Normally, this would take three to four months because our HROD, headed by Willie Cabral, looked for a way to shorten the hiring process.

**[00:28:08.400] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000401)  
Naibaba na namin sa halos 30 days, Your Honor, yung hiring process ng school council associates.

**[00:28:14.400] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000402)  
So, definitely, we're looking at that. We're looking at that. We're looking at that. We're looking at that. We're looking at that. We're looking at that.

**[00:28:16.400] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000403)  
So, definitely, I think dapat palakasin talaga yung approach in schools and building a stronger sense of community, Your Honor.

**[00:28:24.400] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000404)  
So, these are some of the things we're trying to do.

**[00:28:26.400] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000405)  
And of course, training our, kahit yung mga eskwelahan na walang guidance counsellor, which is a systemic problem, as pointed out also by EDCOM.

**[00:28:35.400] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000406)  
E, itatrain na lang sa suho natin sana yung mga guro, how to deal with problems like this.

**[00:28:43.400] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000407)

**[00:28:44.400] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000408)

**[00:29:15.640] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000415)

**[00:29:18.640] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000416)

**[00:38:40.190] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000671)

sa pamilya

**[00:38:47.270] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000675)

**[00:38:49.250] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000676)

**[00:38:51.750] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000677)

**[00:38:53.830] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000678)

**[00:38:55.530] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000679)

**[00:39:01.770] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000682)  
ng kapakanan ng ating

**[00:39:03.270] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000683)  
mga guro. Madam Chair,

**[00:39:05.450] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000684)  
may I just ask a few questions

**[00:39:07.210] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000685)  
on Republic Act 12028

**[00:39:09.890] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000686)  
or the Academic Recovery

**[00:39:11.750] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000687)  
and Accessible Learning

**[00:39:13.430] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000688)  
or the Aral Program Act.

**[00:39:15.890] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000689)  
This was a

**[00:39:17.270] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000690)  
approved on October 16, 2024

**[00:39:19.370] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000691)  
at isa po ako sa

**[00:39:20.810] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000692)  
co-author po dito. And I believe

**[00:39:22.970] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000693)  
that, sekretary, this is one of the most

**[00:39:24.710] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000694)  
important investment we can make

**[00:39:27.210] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000695)  
in our children, sa ating mga kabataang

**[00:39:29.250] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000696)  
mag-aaral. Aware po naman tayo,

**[00:39:32.030] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000697)  
sekretary, na mayroong tinatawag

**[00:39:33.490] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000698)  
na learning gaps

**[00:39:35.210] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000699)  
of our students, which we

**[00:39:37.170] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000700)  
cannot solve by simply moving them

**[00:39:39.370] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000701)  
from one grade level to another.

**[00:39:42.250] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000702)  
Madam Chair,

**[00:39:42.790] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000703)  
a child who reaches

**[00:39:44.810] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000704)  
grade 6, for example,

**[00:39:46.050] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000705)  
without the ability to read

**[00:39:48.270] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000706)  
with comprehension or perform

**[00:39:50.370] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000707)  
basic mathematical

**[00:39:51.810] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000708)  
operations, carries

**[00:39:54.190] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000709)  
that learning deficit

**[00:39:55.450] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000710)  
with him throughout his education.

**[00:39:58.550] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000711)  
Kaya po para sa akin, marapat lamang

**[00:40:00.390] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000712)  
na i-accelerate natin

**[00:40:01.810] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000713)  
ang implementasyon ng aral program.

**[00:40:04.990] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000714)  
Ang magandang programa

**[00:40:06.070] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000715)  
ay dapat lamang nabigyan ng

**[00:40:07.870] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000716)  
kaukulang pondo para magawa

**[00:40:10.210] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000717)  
ito. Sir Sekretary,

**[00:40:12.270] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000718)  
sa DBM's

**[00:40:13.050] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000719)  
Fiscal Year 2027 Budgeting,

**[00:40:16.050] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000720)  
priorities framework specifically identifies the acceleration of the ARAL program as a priority. Tama po ba ito, Secretary? May we know

**[00:40:29.610] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000721)  
the exact amount appropriated for this ARAL program in the 2027 NEP,

**[00:40:33.910] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000722)  
and under what specific program, activity and project or unified accounts structure line item can we find it? Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[00:40:44.690] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000723)  
Maraming salamat,

**[00:40:46.030] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000727)  
Madam Chair. Your Honor, Congressman Pleyto, salamat po sa inyong sinabing magandang salita tungkol sa amin sa DepEd.

**[00:40:54.670] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000728)  
Regarding the ARAL program, there is a zero allotment for this under the 2027 budget.

**[00:41:04.050] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000729)  
Unlike this year where there was an allotment of around 9 billion for the ARAL, the different aspects of the ARAL.

**[00:41:13.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000730)  
So that is why we are requesting, or we proposed rather, during the budget call, 9.98 billion for implementation of the ARAL program.

**[00:41:24.450] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000731)  
Broken down, 1.09 billion for compensation for 15,800 ARAL tutors, 6 billion for learning recovery and remediation consultations,

**[00:41:36.050] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000732)  
1.25 billion for summer program tutors, 1.18 billion for external tutors.

**[00:41:43.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000733)  
And 4.61 billion for ARAL learning resources, and 1.75 billion for the orientation of the tutors, and 94 million for program management, Your Honor.

**[00:41:55.510] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000734)  
At maganda naman ang resulta ng ating programa because doon sa 6.7 million na pumasok po sa ARAL program,

**[00:42:05.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000735)  
we saw at the end of the school year, mayroong 4.5 million fewer struggling readers, at saka may 2.5 million doon sa 6.7 million

**[00:42:13.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000736)  
na umangat po sa grade level reading, so they are now reading at the right level.

**[00:42:20.550] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000737)  
So these are a few of the achievements, at sa mga naikutan kong mga eskwelahan, iyon din ang kinukuwento po ng mga teachers at saka principals na effective po yung ARAL program.

**[00:42:31.990] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000738)  
So again, nagpapasalamat po tayo sa Kongreso sa magandang batas na ito, at hopefully mapagbigyan dito sa mga requests, Your Honor.

**[00:42:40.410] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000739)  
Thank you, Secretary, for that

**[00:42:43.930] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000740)  
response.

**[00:42:45.590] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000741)  
Pangalawa po, Madam Chair.

**[00:42:48.810] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000742)  
Sapat ba ito, Secretary, to cover the whole program and the remuneration of teachers, para-teachers, who will serve as ARAL tutors.

**[00:42:59.570] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000743)  
I believe this covers reading, math, and science.

**[00:43:03.550] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000744)  
Also, these teachers are already carrying a tremendous workload in our public schools.

**[00:43:08.850] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000745)  
If we are asking them to provide additional tutoring,

**[00:43:13.930] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000746)  
beyond their regular responsibilities, we must ensure that their regular responsibilities,

**[00:43:20.470] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000747)  
we must ensure that they are properly and fairly compensated.

**[00:43:25.570] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000748)  
Madam Chair, Secretary, we cannot ask our teachers to close the learning gaps of our children while leaving a gap in the support we give to our teachers.

**[00:43:36.290] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000749)  
Thank you, Madam Chair, Secretary.

**[00:43:38.290] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000750)  
Madam Chair, thank you for the question.

**[00:43:40.870] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000751)  
Kasama po sa request namin iyon, yung remuneration

**[00:43:43.790] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000754)  
ng ARAL teachers and tutors, Your Honor.

**[00:43:46.850] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000755)  
3.5 billion ang total remuneration na ni-request po namin.

**[00:43:51.290] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000756)  
Kasya na ba ito?

**[00:43:53.130] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000757)  
Kasya na po.

**[00:43:56.430] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000758)  
Kasya na po iyon, Your Honor.

**[00:43:58.210] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000759)  
Salamat po sa inyong tugon, Secretary.

**[00:44:00.970] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000760)  
Madam Chair and Secretary, may I manifest my support to allocate appropriate funding needed by DepEd

**[00:44:08.550] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000761)  
to successfully implement the ARAL program.

**[00:44:11.150] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000762)  
It should be our priority to help our Filipino children to catch up,

**[00:44:13.490] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000763)  
learn better

**[00:44:15.930] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000764)  
and regain their confidence

**[00:44:17.830] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000765)  
in their own abilities.

**[00:44:19.490] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-000766)  
Thank you, Madam Chair. Thank you, Secretary.

**[00:44:22.030] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000767)  
Maraming salamat,

**[00:44:23.650] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000768)  
Congressman Pleyto, Madam Chair.

**[00:44:25.550] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000769)  
Salamat po sa support.

**[00:44:27.650] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-000770)  
Thank you very much.

**[00:46:14.940] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-000799)  
Joint delivery voucher for senior high school, technical, vocational, and livelihood specialization, zero na rin po.

**[00:46:22.220] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-000800)  
Anong po ba ang ibig sabihin nito? Maraming pang iba.

**[00:46:27.540] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000801)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Chair, Congressman.

**[00:46:30.600] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000802)  
Thank you very much for the kind words you mentioned.

**[00:46:33.820] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000803)  
Pero tama po kayo, malaki po ang bawas sa budget.

**[00:46:37.040] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000804)  
Pero nantindihan din po natin yung konteksto ng malaking budget ng 2026 na bunga din po,

**[00:46:44.420] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000805)  
kumbaga bonanza ng flood control dahil yung 200 billion na napunta po sa edukasyon at health.

**[00:46:54.200] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000806)  
And hopefully, magpatuloy po.

**[00:46:55.780] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000807)  
So, tama po kayo na nawala po yung mga pondong yun.

**[00:47:00.600] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000808)  
And so, yung sa feeding, for example, nag-request po kami ng additional para ma-maintain lang po yung this year's level.

**[00:47:09.820] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000809)  
Kasi this year, aside from universal kindergarten o lahat ng bata sa kindergarten ay pinapakain, pati grade 1 po.

**[00:47:18.080] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000810)  
So, napansin nga ng mga UNICEF at saka ng mga ibang international organizations na yung commitment ng Pilipinas sa foundational learning

**[00:47:28.220] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000811)  
and holistic learning.

**[00:47:30.600] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000812)  
Holistic approach to learning.

**[00:47:32.760] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000813)  
So, sana yun, maibalik po.

**[00:47:35.160] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000814)  
At kasi ang sakop din po ng feeding ngayon is 120 days under the 2027 budget.

**[00:47:42.480] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000815)  
Hopefully, ma-extend yun sa 180 days, which is for the current school year or the current fiscal year.

**[00:47:49.540] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000816)  
Kasi nga po, ang napansin namin is, tinitimbang natin yung mga bata at very successful yung feeding dahil,

**[00:48:00.600] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000817)  
they gain weight.

**[00:48:03.460] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000818)  
Pero, pagka meron pong butal or meron pong portion ng school year na hindi sila pinapakain,

**[00:48:08.840] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000819)  
bumabalik din po sa dating tinbang.

**[00:48:11.180] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000820)  
And, of course, the evidence is very strong also for improved learning outcomes,

**[00:48:19.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000821)  
especially sa younger years.

**[00:48:21.520] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000822)  
That's why we're focusing on kinder and grade 1.

**[00:48:23.980] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000823)  
So, sana may balik po yun.

**[00:48:25.720] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000824)  
Definitely, there's a proven connection or correlation between the feeding and the,

**[00:48:30.560] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000825)  
the learning outcomes, Your Honor.

**[00:48:31.780] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000826)  
So, that's just one aspect of the many programs that you mentioned.

**[00:48:36.980] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000827)  
So, yung aral nga, as mentioned in the interchange with Congressman Plato,

**[00:48:41.340] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000828)  
wala rin pondo, allotted funds for that.

**[00:48:43.880] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000829)  
So, and then, ang gusto nga po natin is ma-expand yung aral program to math and science later on.

**[00:48:51.100] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000830)  
Dahil yung nagawa po lang natin is reading, aral reading.

**[00:48:55.220] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000831)  
And yet, napakaganda na po nung epekto.

**[00:48:57.640] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000832)  
Dahil for the first time in history,

**[00:48:59.360] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000833)  
at nagpapasalamat muli tayo sa Kongreso kay Pangulong Marcos,

**[00:49:02.880] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000834)  
na merong programa, na merong tutors ang bawat estudyante ng public schools.

**[00:49:07.980] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000835)  
First time nangyari po yan.

**[00:49:09.520] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000836)  
I know certain LGUs, certain schools, kumukuha ng sponsor para gawin yun.

**[00:49:16.820] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000837)  
Pero it's the first time it was done on a system-wide basis.

**[00:49:19.840] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000838)  
So, sana masustain yung momentum na ito.

**[00:49:21.940] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000839)  
And at the same time, it's also,

**[00:49:25.300] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000840)  
it's also an incentive, yung school feeding is also an incentive for attendance.

**[00:49:31.320] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000841)  
Nakita natin, nag-improve din po yung attendance ng mga bata sa eskwelahan.

**[00:49:35.760] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000842)  
So, these are just two of the aspects of the, of, tapos yung voucher program,

**[00:49:41.620] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000843)  
hindi naman na zero yung voucher program.

**[00:49:46.800] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000844)  
Ano lang, nabawasan lang ng konti.

**[00:49:48.440] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000845)  
So, that means less beneficiaries also.

**[00:49:51.080] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000846)  
And as we know, the voucher program is also one way of decongesting

**[00:49:57.180] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000847)  
our congested public schools

**[00:49:57.340] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000848)  
by giving vouchers to nearby private schools in the area

**[00:50:03.720] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000849)  
para mabawasan po ng class size yung ating mga ibang public school, Your Honor.

**[00:50:09.680] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-000850)

**[00:50:15.100] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-000851)

**[00:50:23.550] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000853)

**[00:50:28.370] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000854)

**[00:50:32.290] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000855)

for educational services.

Billion.

parami pong pangako ang Pangulo sa edukasyon.

**[00:51:44.380] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-000875)  
Tutugunan ang kakulangan at kondisyon ng mga silid-aralan.

**[00:51:48.740] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-000876)

Magtatayo ng 40,000 classrooms bago matapos ang termino sa 2028.

Classrooms, o?

**[00:52:01.790] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000880)  
Classrooms?

Yes.

**[00:52:02.990] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-000882)

**[00:52:06.110] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-000883)  
Bago matapos ang termino sa 2028.

**[00:52:16.170] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000887)

**[00:52:22.770] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000888)

**[00:52:27.770] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000889)

**[00:52:32.630] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000890)  
So, that will...

**[00:52:36.110] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000891)  
represent around 18,000 classrooms, Your Honor.

**[00:52:43.140] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000892)

**[00:52:47.500] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000893)  
which na-approve na ho ng NEDA or DepDev ICC,

**[00:52:53.180] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000894)  
yung...

**[00:52:54.520] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000895)  
How many?

**[00:52:57.700] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000896)  
Another 16,000, ho.

**[00:52:59.680] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000897)  
So, dito pa lang sa taong ito, ma-umpisahan na ho siguro yung 34,000.

**[00:53:04.480] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000898)  
So, it's still a long way towards the...

**[00:53:09.120] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000899)  
140 plus thousand classroom deficit that we have accumulated over the years.

**[00:53:15.700] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000900)

**[00:53:26.160] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000901)  
So, kumbaga hindi siya business as usual, Your Honor, Mr. President.

**[00:53:31.120] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000902)  
Madam Chair, talagang nag-i-effort si Pangulo and ang member ng Kongreso to solve this...

**[00:53:39.120] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000903)  
years-old problem, Kong Egay.

**[00:53:43.240] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-000904)  
Yes, thank you, Secretary.

**[00:53:45.740] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-000905)

**[00:53:49.380] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-000906)

**[00:53:53.760] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-000907)

**[00:53:57.220] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000908)

**[00:54:00.200] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-000909)  
Bibigyan ng laptop ang bawat public school teacher.

**[00:54:06.080] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000910)  
Well, this year, we're giving...

**[00:54:10.120] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000911)  
I think over 200,000...

**[00:54:12.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000912)  
283,000 laptops, which, again, is not business as usual, Your Honor.

**[00:54:18.270] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000913)  
We have about 870,000 teachers.

**[00:54:21.810] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000914)  
This is the biggest single allocation in any year for our teachers.

**[00:54:26.730] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000915)  
So, again, the question is sustainability.

**[00:54:29.610] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000916)  
Dahil kung hindi natin masustain ito, hindi natin mabibigyan lahat.

**[00:54:33.370] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000917)  
But definitely, in one year, we're giving almost one-third of the school teachers already.

**[00:54:41.190] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-000918)  
So, by end of the term, the President, kaya-kayang mabibigyan?

**[00:54:45.070] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-000919)  
Lahat ng laptop, yung mga teachers?

**[00:54:49.730] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000920)  
Sa 2027 budget, there's funding only for 50,000 laptops, Your Honor.

**[00:54:56.130] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000921)  
That's why we're requesting an additional 10B para ma-fulfill po yung pangako niya.

**[00:55:03.630] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-000922)  
How about yung pangako po na yung lowering of learning poverty para po sa ating basic education?

**[00:55:11.670] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-000923)  
Ano na po ba ang improvement?

**[00:55:12.750] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-000924)  
For example, in the...

**[00:55:15.070] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000925)  
Well, this week, hopefully, we will be getting some good news.

**[00:55:31.730] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000926)

**[00:55:39.170] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-000927)  
Hanggang bukas po.

**[00:55:39.970] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000928)  
Opo.

**[00:55:40.270] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000929)  
Dahil iuunang ng OECD yung pag-release po ng impormasyon.

**[00:55:44.150] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-000930)  
Kasi po, yung OECD standards, nung mga nakaraan, nasa mga 69% yung average sa mga kabataan sa buong mundo,

**[00:56:00.910] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-000931)  
tayo po, napakalayo natin. Hopefully, tomorrow, we will hear good news.

**[00:56:08.750] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000932)  
Yes, Your Honor. Hopefully.

**[00:56:09.910] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000933)  
Because the department has really been making an effort.

**[00:56:13.490] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000934)  
At mismo si Pangulo.

**[00:56:14.150] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000935)

**[00:56:19.950] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000936)  
At you're right. In 2018, we were the last of all the countries, I think of about 60 plus countries who participated.

**[00:56:27.410] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000937)

**[00:56:31.190] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000938)  
So, hopefully...

**[00:56:32.810] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000939)  
Hopefully, wala na tayo sa third, second, at last.

**[00:56:39.410] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-000940)  
Anyway, anyway po.

**[00:56:40.910] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-000941)  
So, pagdating naman po sa mga textbooks.

**[00:56:44.150] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-000942)  
And materials. Ano po ba ang improvement natin dyan?

**[00:56:48.190] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000943)  
Again, Madam Chair, there is a historic allocation of over 20 billion for textbooks this year.

**[00:56:56.010] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000944)  
So, yung pangarap po natin is, yung naabutan po kasi namin, yung isang textbook ay sineshare at pinapasa ng apat na batches ng mga bata.

**[00:57:06.970] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000945)  
Kaya sinasabihan yung bata, huwag sulatan yung textbook, huwag iuwi dahil ipapasa pa niya yun.

**[00:57:13.130] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000946)  
So, minsan, kung ikaw yung fourth batch, ang nakuha mo na ay warak-warak na po na libro.

**[00:57:18.790] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000947)  
So, ang goal talaga natin is one textbook, one learner, your honor.

**[00:57:23.610] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000948)  
And we're on our way to meet that, so definitely, it is not business as usual.

**[00:57:28.070] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000949)  
At nadagdagan din po natin yung supplementary learning materials, which is not just the textbooks na dun sa mga klase ng mga bata.

**[00:57:36.390] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000950)  
Pati yung mga libro na nasa library, at pwede silang iuwi-iwi ito.

**[00:57:40.010] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000951)  
Dahil dati, hindi na iuwi ito dahil...

**[00:57:43.130] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000952)  
Saka teacher pag nawawala, so ayaw na teacher pa uwi.

**[00:57:46.450] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000953)  
Pero ngayon, hindi na natin ganun ang polisiya.

**[00:57:48.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000954)  
They're free to bring it home and they're free to write also.

**[00:57:54.130] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000955)  
We're encouraging them to write because there are studies which show that writing, handwriting aids memory retention, your honor.

**[00:58:02.530] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000956)  
That's one of the biggest studies which came out recently.

**[00:58:07.350] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000957)  
So, these are some of the developments in textbooks, your honor.

**[00:58:10.370] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000958)  
At dati...

**[00:58:13.130] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000959)

**[00:58:21.970] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000960)  
In 2026, we procured 105 out of 105 titles, your honor.

**[00:58:28.090] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000961)  
Ang challenge na lang namin is i-deliver on time.

**[00:58:30.650] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000962)  
Dati nalilate ng one year.

**[00:58:32.190] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000963)

**[00:58:35.250] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000964)  
So, yun. Pero I think before 2028, makuha na natin yun because we are working on early procurement, your honor.

**[00:58:43.130] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-000965)  
So, 37 seconds na lang ako.

**[00:58:45.950] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-000966)

Negative apps or websites that have a negative influence on him.

**[00:59:31.490] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000976)  
So, this has led many countries, in fact, to impose social media bans, your honor.

**[00:59:36.230] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-000977)  
Maraming salamat po and congratulations to the DepEd family.

**[00:59:39.610] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000978)  
Maraming salamat sa inyo, Kong Egy.

**[00:59:41.070] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-000979)  
Madam Chair, thank you.

**[00:59:43.130] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-000980)

Thank you very much Madam Chair.

**[01:00:53.210] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000997)  
Magandang umaga po sa buong DepEd family.

**[01:00:55.930] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000998)  
Magandang umaga po sa lahat.

**[01:00:57.730] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000999)  
Maraming salamat po Secretary at sa buong DepEd

**[01:01:00.070] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001000)  
sa lahat ng mga ginagawa niyo.

**[01:01:01.390] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001001)  
Kanina nga nabanggit niyo yung mga record figures

**[01:01:04.730] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001002)  
dun sa mga sa classroom,

**[01:01:06.550] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001003)  
sa mga at saka pinamamahagi niyo ng mga materials.

**[01:01:09.330] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001004)  
Thank you for your efforts.

**[01:01:11.310] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001005)  
Unfortunately, sabi niyo nga,

**[01:01:13.210] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001006)  
hindi pa enough.

**[01:01:14.050] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001007)  
Marami pang kulang.

**[01:01:15.090] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001008)  
At least, unti-unti na atutugunan natin.

**[01:01:18.190] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001009)  
Representing a poverty stricken district and province,

**[01:01:22.130] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001010)  
malaking bagay po sa amin yung sitwasyon

**[01:01:23.870] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001011)  
ng classroom shortage.

**[01:01:26.730] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001012)  
Lalo sa distrito ko,

**[01:01:28.210] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001013)  
more than 700 pa yung shortage namin.

**[01:01:30.390] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001014)  
Tapos yung needing

**[01:01:31.390] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001015)  
minor and major repairs,

**[01:01:32.750] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001016)  
more than a thousand.

**[01:01:33.950] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001017)  
Gusto lang po naman kung ano yung updates

**[01:01:35.950] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001018)  
tungkol doon sa classrooms.

**[01:01:39.630] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001019)  
We have signed MOAS with

**[01:01:44.430] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001020)  
about over 350 local government units,

**[01:01:47.970] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001021)  
Your Honor.

**[01:01:48.710] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001022)  
Representing over 20 billion pesos worth of classrooms.

**[01:01:52.390] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001023)  
Tapos yung

**[01:01:54.390] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001024)  
nabigay na rin po namin yung validated lists of

**[01:01:58.050] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001025)  
ang good news siguro is that

**[01:02:09.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001026)  
the next batch of public-private partnership

**[01:02:13.420] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001027)  
will have a big focus on Mindanao.

**[01:02:15.900] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001028)  
So we'll make sure that your district is included

**[01:02:18.620] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001029)  
because 700 is a big backlog for one district,

**[01:02:21.560] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001030)  
Your Honor.

**[01:02:22.260] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001031)  
At we acknowledge that

**[01:02:23.520] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001032)  
Zamboanga Peninsula is one of the

**[01:02:26.120] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001033)  
most poverty stricken areas of our country

**[01:02:28.600] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001034)  
and we will make an effort to

**[01:02:30.020] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001035)  
really improve the educational situation there.

**[01:02:33.360] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001036)  
Given that you don't have many cities,

**[01:02:35.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001037)  
hindi mayayaman yung mga LGUs doon.

**[01:02:37.280] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001038)  
So talagang, you're very reliant on national

**[01:02:39.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001039)  
government support.

**[01:02:41.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001040)  
We acknowledge that, Your Honor.

**[01:02:43.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001041)  
And we will take that into account in the funding of classrooms in the future, Madam Chair, Your Honor.

**[01:02:49.580] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001042)  
Knowing fully yung problema natin sa classrooms,

**[01:02:52.580] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001043)  
thankful tayo na maraming ginagawa.

**[01:02:55.580] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001044)  
Pwede po kayang ma-increase natin yung budget natin for repairs of classrooms?

**[01:02:59.580] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001045)  
Kasi minsan may mga classrooms na needing repair,

**[01:03:02.580] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001046)  
eh pag hindi natin ni-repair, eh tutuloy lang masisira yun.

**[01:03:05.580] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001047)  
So magco-construct naman tayo ng bago.

**[01:03:07.580] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001048)  
Since mas mura naman mag-repair,

**[01:03:09.580] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001049)  
lalong-lalo na po sa mga poverty stricken na mga provinces.

**[01:03:15.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001050)  
Maganda po yung puntos ninyo, Madam Chair, Your Honor.

**[01:03:18.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001051)  
Kasi madalas nababanggit yung backlog sa classrooms.

**[01:03:22.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001052)  
Pero may backlog din tayo sa repairs.

**[01:03:26.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001053)  
In fact, the backlog for repairs is probably more than the...

**[01:03:29.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001054)  
And why is that?

**[01:03:30.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001055)  
Because many of our classrooms are over 50 years old already.

**[01:03:34.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001056)  
Kumbaga past the use date already.

**[01:03:37.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001057)  
So pinagamit na lang natin dahil wala tayong...

**[01:03:39.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001058)  
Pinagtsatsaga na lang po natin dahil wala pa po yung bagong classroom.

**[01:03:43.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001059)  
So tama po kayo kung pwede po madagdagan.

**[01:03:46.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001060)  
Again, for this year, napakagandang budget po.

**[01:03:48.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001061)  
I think we had about 6 or 7 billion for repairs.

**[01:03:51.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001062)  
So marami po tayong na-repair.

**[01:03:53.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001063)  
But I think for 2027...

**[01:03:58.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001064)  
There's 5 billion for the budget, Your Honor.

**[01:04:00.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001065)  
So that's still higher than previous years, Your Honor.

**[01:04:02.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001066)  
Where ang budget for repairs was just a few billion.

**[01:04:05.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001067)  
So kung madagdagan pa ho, we would welcome that.

**[01:04:08.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001068)  
Because as mentioned, talagang hindi napapansin masyado yung repairs deficit actually.

**[01:04:14.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001069)  
Where we are holding off on condemning school buildings which already should be condemned.

**[01:04:20.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001070)  
And perhaps are not as safe as they should be.

**[01:04:23.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001071)  
Kung tinamaan po tayo ng mga sakuna, like nilindol po tayo,

**[01:04:27.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001072)  
baka we will have unintended consequences, Your Honor, Madam Chair.

**[01:04:35.580] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001073)  
Madam Chair, isa sa mga problema namin, being very far from Metro Manila,

**[01:04:44.930] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001074)  
is yung mga materials natin dun sa amin, ang tagal dumating.

**[01:04:47.930] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001075)  
So minsan, kawawa yung mga estudyante, medyo dehado na nga sa infrastructure.

**[01:04:53.930] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001076)  
Dun ba sa learning materials, minsan ang tagal-tagal pang dumating.

**[01:04:57.930] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001077)  
Baka po pwedeng ma-address natin?

**[01:04:59.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001078)  
Yes, Your Honor. We're undertaking early procurement.

**[01:05:02.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001079)  
And as mentioned, we've closed the gap in procuring textbooks.

**[01:05:05.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001080)  
We're trying to close the gap in delivering textbooks on time, Your Honor.

**[01:05:09.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001081)  
Pero dati, buwan at saka taon ang delay sa textbooks.

**[01:05:13.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001082)  
Ngayon, linggo na lang. Pero unacceptable pa rin ho ito.

**[01:05:16.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001083)  
We acknowledge and we will strive to do better, Your Honor.

**[01:05:20.930] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001084)  
Last po, yung ano, meron po ba tayong program yung sa...

**[01:05:25.930] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001085)  
Kasi mga teachers natin, karamihan, although pinataasan natin yung sahod,

**[01:05:29.930] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001086)  
eh lagi namang problema natin yung sa mga pautang.

**[01:05:33.930] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001087)  
So yung meron po ba tayong program yung sa...

**[01:05:34.930] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001088)  
So yung meron ba tayong program para ma-educate yung teachers natin?

**[01:05:38.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001089)  
Yes, meron po tayong financial literacy programs that we encourage teachers.

**[01:05:44.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001090)  
But of course, every month may trainings po tayo.

**[01:05:48.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001091)  
Pero siyempre, yung kultura nandun pa rin.

**[01:05:50.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001092)  
At saka siyempre, ang kaibahan po ng teacher talagang hindi lang mga anak o asawang dependent sa kanya.

**[01:05:57.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001093)  
Pati yung buong angkan, minsan nakaangkas dun sa ating mga guro.

**[01:06:02.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001094)  
I think that's well known to our honorable legislators.

**[01:06:06.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001095)  
But having said that, we're trying to find ways also.

**[01:06:09.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001096)  
Isa dito is yung in-extend po natin yung maturity nung allowable loans.

**[01:06:14.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001097)  
Dati-rati, five years ang maximum nung pwedeng i-payroll deduction.

**[01:06:20.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001098)  
So hindi lumalampas ng five years.

**[01:06:22.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001099)  
Sumulat po tayo sa BSP na i-extend po ito.

**[01:06:25.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001100)  
Kaya pinayagan po ng BSP.

**[01:06:27.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001101)  
Kaya seven years.

**[01:06:28.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001102)  
Binaba po nung maraming financial institution o banko,

**[01:06:31.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001103)  
ang nagbaba ng kanilang interest.

**[01:06:33.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001104)  
At tumagal din po yung payment.

**[01:06:34.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001105)  
Dahil makikita naman po natin, tumatagal din po ang ating mga teachers sa tenure nila.

**[01:06:40.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001106)  
Many stay 10, 20, 30 years.

**[01:06:42.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001107)  
So this is a more realistic option for them financially, Your Honor.

**[01:06:48.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001108)  
Madam Chair.

**[01:06:49.930] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001109)  
Lastly po, yung nabanggit kanina ni Kong Erice na 150 ba yung backlog niya.

**[01:06:57.930] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001110)  
Medyo naingit ako kasi yung sa amin 700 eh.

**[01:06:59.930] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001111)  
Madam Chair.

**[01:07:00.930] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001112)  
Hindi kaya pwedeng yung mga mas maraming kulang eh mas maraming ma-allocate na school buildings?

**[01:07:06.930] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001113)  
Kasi minsan naingit kami yung ibang tao na wala, ibang distrito na konting-konti lang pare-pareho kami ng allocation every year eh.

**[01:07:13.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001114)  
That's a good point, Your Honor.

**[01:07:15.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001115)  
We will look into that and we'll work with you on that, Madam Chair, Your Honor.

**[01:07:19.930] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001116)  
Thank you very much, Madam Chair.

**[01:07:21.930] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001117)  
And good luck sa DepEd family.

**[01:07:22.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001118)  
Maraming salamat sa suporta, Kong Adrian.

**[01:07:24.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001119)  
Thank you po.

**[01:07:25.930] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-001120)  
Thank you.

**[01:07:27.930] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-001121)  
Next to ask question is from the minority.

**[01:07:29.930] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-001122)  
The Chair acknowledges Representative Antonio L. Tinio.

**[01:07:34.930] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001123)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[01:07:36.930] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001124)  
Good morning po sa DepEd, kay Secretary Angara.

**[01:07:40.930] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001125)  
Unang tanong tungkol po sa expanded career progression.

**[01:07:43.930] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001126)  
So, from the field ang nakakarating po sa atin

**[01:07:48.930] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001127)  
ng implementation and funding ng ECP 2025-2026.

**[01:07:56.930] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001128)  
Ilan pa po yung backlog?

**[01:07:58.930] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001129)  
Ilan pa po yung backlog na qualified na pero hindi pa napopromote dahil walang budget?

**[01:08:06.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001130)  
Your Honor, Madam Chair, magandang umaga, Kong Tonjie.

**[01:08:11.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001131)  
For 2026, there is a 6 billion budget given by Congress.

**[01:08:15.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001132)  
And this is enough to promote 82,000 of our teachers and school heads, Your Honor.

**[01:08:22.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001133)  
And in 2027, there is a 12 billion budget.

**[01:08:25.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001134)  
And this should answer for the promotions of 135,000 school heads.

**[01:08:30.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001135)  
So, hindi po equal yung...

**[01:08:32.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001136)  
Kasi yung minsan, sa tagal nang hindi sila napromote,

**[01:08:35.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001137)  
they are not only being promoted ng isang rangpo,

**[01:08:39.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001138)  
hindi minsan dalawa, tatlo.

**[01:08:41.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001139)  
So, teacher 1, nagiging teacher 4.

**[01:08:43.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001140)  
Teacher 4, nagiging teacher...

**[01:08:45.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001141)  
We made new positions, Your Honor.

**[01:08:48.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001142)  
So, teacher 6, gano'n minsan may gano'n.

**[01:08:50.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001143)  
Hindi lang isang rangpo po.

**[01:08:52.930] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001144)  
So, 82,000 promotions were...

**[01:08:53.930] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001145)  
82,000 promotions were funded for 2026.

**[01:08:59.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001146)  
That's correct, Your Honor.

**[01:09:00.930] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001147)  
Pero ilan yung nag-iintay pa ng funding?

**[01:09:04.930] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001148)  
Qualified na pero nag-iintay pa ng funding?

**[01:09:07.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001149)  
Mga, there are about 100,000 still waiting and entitled to the promotions, Your Honor.

**[01:09:17.250] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001150)  
Okay. So, in other words, may qualified na sila, 100,000.

**[01:09:23.250] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001151)  
They went through the process pero nag-iintay pa ng funding.

**[01:09:26.250] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001152)  
So...

**[01:09:28.250] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001153)  
Kailan po sila mapupondohan?

**[01:09:31.250] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001154)  
In 2027, Your Honor, because we only have 6 billion for this year, Your Honor.

**[01:09:36.250] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001155)  
Okay. So, again, for 2027, ang target niyo po ay...

**[01:09:41.250] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001156)  
That's good for...

**[01:09:43.250] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001157)  
135,000, Your Honor.

**[01:09:45.250] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001158)  
135,000. So, yung 100,000 dun ay para lang dun sa backlog.

**[01:09:51.250] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001159)  
Opo.

**[01:09:52.250] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001160)  
So, in effect, for 2027, the new promotions will be for 35,000.

**[01:09:57.250] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001161)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[01:19:38.560] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001399)

**[01:59:00.260] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002354)  
assessments like the PISA, because if you

**[01:59:02.600] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002355)  
read the PISA report from 2018

**[01:59:04.460] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002356)  
and 2022, very few of our

**[01:59:06.740] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002357)  
learners had access to computers

**[01:59:08.420] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002358)  
at that. And this is a computer

**[01:59:10.500] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002359)  
administered exam, your honor.

**[01:59:13.040] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002360)  
So, some of the efforts of DEPED

**[01:59:14.660] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002361)  
have been geared towards

**[01:59:15.980] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002362)  
providing

**[01:59:17.060] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002363)  
computer access to our

**[01:59:20.440] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002364)  
learners.

**[01:59:20.920] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-002365)  
Should, yes, Mr. Secretary, so that's

**[01:59:22.860] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-002366)  
about, what, 10% of the

**[01:59:24.780] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-002367)  
proposed budget? 967

**[01:59:26.920] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-002368)  
billion, so more or less?

**[01:59:31.400] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002369)  
It's about 4%

**[01:59:32.660] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002370)  
of the DEPED budget, your honor, because

**[01:59:34.780] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002371)  
personnel services, about

**[01:59:36.780] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002372)  
79%. So, if I'm

**[01:59:38.800] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002373)  
talking about the learning systems and curriculum

**[01:59:40.880] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002374)  
strand, that's about 4%, your honor.

**[01:59:43.140] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-002375)  
Okay. Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:59:45.020] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-002376)  
My next question would be

**[01:59:46.780] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-002377)  
how do you

**[01:59:48.700] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-002378)  
evaluate whether interventional

**[01:59:50.920] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-002379)  
intervention, your interventions, are

**[01:59:52.700] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-002380)  
producing actual improvements?

**[01:59:54.820] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-002381)  
What tools exactly do

**[01:59:56.760] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-002382)  
you use? A baseline assessment

**[01:59:58.680] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-002383)  
tool do you use?

**[02:00:00.000] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-002384)  
to gauge or measure our improvements?

**[02:00:04.280] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002385)  
We have the CRLA.

**[02:00:06.320] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002386)  
This is an assessment tool to measure the reading.

**[02:00:11.680] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002387)  
This is the Comprehensive Rapid Literacy Assessment.

**[02:00:15.180] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002388)  
We also have the ELNA, or the Early Language Literacy

**[02:00:18.700] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002389)  
and Numeracy Assessment.

**[02:00:21.400] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002390)  
This is the assessment tool which

**[02:00:25.200] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002391)  
looks at the learner's foundational skills

**[02:00:27.160] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002392)  
in early language, literacy, and numeracy at the end

**[02:00:30.820] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002393)  
of Key Stage 1, or grade 3.

**[02:00:33.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002394)  
So as mentioned, we have the CRLA.

**[02:00:35.260] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002395)  
Then we have the Philippine Informal Reading Inventory,

**[02:00:39.660] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002396)  
or FIL-IRI, and Rapid Math Assessment, or RMA.

**[02:00:44.260] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002397)  
These are administered at beginning and the end

**[02:00:46.640] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002398)  
of school year to assess the literacy and numeracy

**[02:00:49.560] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002399)  
of each learner per grade level from grades 1 to 10.

**[02:00:52.780] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002400)  
And the literacy and numeracy knowledge link tests

**[02:00:56.200] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002401)  
are now being piloted.

**[02:00:57.140] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002402)  
This is a harmonized classroom-based assessment

**[02:01:00.860] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002403)  
system from grades 1 to 12 that will

**[02:01:03.080] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002404)  
enable consistent tracking of learner progress in literacy

**[02:01:06.960] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002405)  
and numeracy over time.

**[02:01:09.580] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-002406)  
Thank you, Mr. Secretary.

**[02:01:12.080] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-002407)  
My next question is going to be on digitalization, ICT,

**[02:01:16.520] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-002408)  
and e-learning infrastructure.

**[02:01:20.200] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-002409)  
One of the audit observations of COA for 2025 is,

**[02:01:26.640] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-002410)  
on the computerization program packages,

**[02:01:31.080] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-002411)  
and for 2027, you are again requesting 5.8 billion

**[02:01:36.640] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-002412)  
for computerization program.

**[02:01:40.980] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-002413)  
How do we, perhaps, ,,

**[02:01:45.100] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-002414)  
what lessons have we learned from the previous ICT

**[02:01:49.800] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-002415)  
implementation failures of that?

**[02:01:52.980] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002416)  
Well, we've addressed that COA, and these computers have been

**[02:01:56.180] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002417)  
fully delivered already, Your Honor.

**[02:01:57.820] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002418)  
So these are just snapshots of a moment in time,

**[02:02:01.060] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002419)  
and we've addressed that.

**[02:02:03.360] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002420)  
So we're also improving our tracking system

**[02:02:06.500] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002421)  
so we know exactly when the computers are

**[02:02:09.260] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002422)  
in the hands of our schools and learners, Your Honor.

**[02:02:12.860] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-002423)  
OK, thank you, Mr. Secretary.

**[02:02:15.260] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-002424)  
The next question will be on last mile schools.

**[02:02:20.400] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-002425)

**[02:02:24.660] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-002426)

For 2027.

**[02:02:50.140] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-002436)  
OK.

**[02:02:51.140] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-002437)  
Since I still have a few minutes left, ang sunod ko po sigurong katanungan,

**[02:02:55.620] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-002438)

**[02:03:25.620] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002445)

Thank you very much.

I will be glad to receive such information as soon as time

**[02:03:36.340] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-002449)  
possible.

**[02:03:36.840] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-002450)  
Thank you.

**[02:03:43.180] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-002452)

**[02:04:36.240] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-002471)  
Next to ask question is Representative Nelson Legacion.

**[02:04:42.500] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002473)

**[02:04:49.000] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002475)

**[02:04:51.580] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002476)

DepEd Budget Requests.

**[02:05:37.360] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002486)  
Okay.

**[02:05:38.340] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002487)  
Sa akin po, walang problema po yun.

**[02:05:40.060] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002488)  
Basta may mapagkukuhaan.

**[02:05:41.340] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002489)  
And I would like to, this early,

**[02:05:45.640] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002490)  
I would like to manifest that I am in full support of this request for additional budget.

**[02:05:54.180] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002491)  
Now, I will be further talking about,

**[02:05:58.880] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002492)  
ah, two matters which I often experience when I was a local chief executive.

**[02:06:09.580] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002493)  
I am referring to the maintenance and other operating expenses and capital outlay.

**[02:06:17.160] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002494)  
Pero yung sa capital outlay, kahit hindi na po siguro kasi marami na pong nagtanong.

**[02:06:23.540] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002495)  
Pagdating doon po sa MOOE, napansin ko lang po ano,

**[02:06:28.880] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002496)  
under the 2026 GAA, nasa 207 billion po siya.

**[02:06:34.260] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002497)  
Pero sa proposed 2027 budget, nasa 174 billion.

**[02:06:41.080] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002498)  
May difference of 33 billion pesos.

**[02:06:46.960] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002499)  
Ang nakita ko rin po, from doon sa elementary,

**[02:06:52.340] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002500)  
yung appropriation per learner for the elementary learners,

**[02:06:56.860] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002501)  
dati nasa 2,140.

**[02:07:00.440] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002502)  
Pero ngayong taon, 2,792.

**[02:07:04.520] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002503)  
For high school learners, from 2,784, ngayon ay 3,264.

**[02:07:13.640] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002504)  
Dahil bumaba po yung MOOE, ang ibig bang sabihin noon,

**[02:07:18.200] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002505)  
number one, nagkamali ba tayo sa paghingi ng mataas na budget for MOOE under 2026 GAA?

**[02:07:26.540] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002506)  
Pangalawa,

**[02:07:26.860] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002507)  
yung pagbaba ng MOOE by 33 billion,

**[02:07:34.740] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002508)  
hindi kaya maapektuhan ito yung pangailangan ng bawat paaralan?

**[02:07:43.410] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002509)  
Tama po kayo, Your Honor, Kong Nelson.

**[02:07:47.270] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002510)  
Yung mas malaki ho yung allotment for capital outlay and MOOE for 2026 kumpara sa 2027.

**[02:07:54.730] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002511)  
Parang kinainan ng personal services representing almost 79% of the total.

**[02:08:00.090] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002512)  
Whereas for this year, it's about 60 plus percent, Your Honor.

**[02:08:03.870] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002513)  
Pero yung concern ko po yung pagbaba noong MOOE from 207 billion to 174 billion.

**[02:08:15.610] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002514)  
Pero yung numero naman po ng ating estudyante, halos hindi naman po nagbabago, diba?

**[02:08:21.790] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002515)  
And correspondingly, kapag binabaan mo po yung MOOE,

**[02:08:25.350] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002516)  
bababa yung allocation per learner.

**[02:08:28.770] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002517)  
Bababa yung...

**[02:08:30.090] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002518)  
Yung ibinababa po nating budget sa bawat paaralan.

**[02:08:33.750] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002519)  
Tama po sila. Apektado po yun.

**[02:08:37.110] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002520)  
And these items include school feeding, textbooks.

**[02:08:41.130] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002521)  
So to the extent na nabawasan yun, tama po kayo.

**[02:08:43.770] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002522)  
Yung allocation per learner is affected.

**[02:08:46.710] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002523)  
So given that, hindi ba maaring maiakyat ulit, maibalik sa dati?

**[02:08:52.190] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002524)  
Nasa wish list po namin yun at nasa kamay na po ng mga kongresista.

**[02:08:55.610] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002525)  
Kasama na po ba yan doon sa 145 billion?

**[02:08:58.390] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002526)  
Kasama po.

**[02:08:58.910] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002527)  
Kasama na po yan.

**[02:08:59.770] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002528)  
Kasama po.

**[02:09:00.090] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002529)  
Okay, sige.

**[02:09:01.390] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002530)  
Last point na lang po, Madam Chairman.

**[02:09:06.070] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002531)  
Patungkol po sa Special Education Fund,

**[02:09:10.070] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002532)

**[02:09:14.130] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002533)

**[02:09:17.430] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002534)  
Kapag sinumada mo.

**[02:09:19.350] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002535)  
Ano yun?

**[02:09:22.750] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002536)
