**[02:09:25.530] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002537)  
Pero ang tanong ko, yung gamit po ba nito, ay nasusubaybayan ba yan?

**[02:09:30.090] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002538)  
Sa Department of Education.

**[02:09:31.670] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002539)  
Kasi sa tingin ko, yung paggamit po ng Special Education Fund,

**[02:09:35.570] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002540)  
kinakailangang naka-align po yun sa programs and projects ng Department of Education.

**[02:09:41.530] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002541)  
At tinitingnan dapat kung paano ito nakakatulong sa goals, objective ng Department of Education.

**[02:09:51.670] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002542)  
At tama po kayo, Your Honor, na mahalaga yung SCF at dapat naka-align yun sa mga layunin ng DepEd.

**[02:10:00.090] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002543)  
But unfortunately, there's such a thing as local autonomy, diba?

**[02:10:03.450] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002544)  
So mixed experiences ang DepEd ho dyan.

**[02:10:06.730] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002545)  
Pag medyo forward-looking at enlightened yung local chief executive,

**[02:10:12.770] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002546)  
pinapaubayan na po sa DepEd ang paggamit po ng Special Education Fund.

**[02:10:17.870] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002547)  
Pero having said that, pinalawak na po natin yung menu ng Special Education Fund

**[02:10:22.590] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002548)  
para hindi lang yung traditional items ang pwedeng pondohan.

**[02:10:27.070] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002549)  
But again, yung talagang coercive

**[02:10:30.090] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002550)  
power over the use of the SEF is not with DepEd.

**[02:10:33.150] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002551)  
It's probably with DOF if it lies with any agency.

**[02:10:36.170] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002552)  
Kasi hindi naman kami yung nagpamonitor doon.

**[02:10:39.010] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002553)  
At yung ibang, I'm aware that some LGUs, yung SCF allotment for that year,

**[02:10:46.570] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002554)  
hindi nila ginagastos, minsan iniipon po nila yun.

**[02:10:49.830] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002555)  
Yun lang po, Madam Chairman.

**[02:10:51.250] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002556)  
At muli, yung pagpapayag po ng suporta sa Department of Education,

**[02:10:57.090] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002557)  
including the request for additional budget.

**[02:11:00.090] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002558)  
Yes, sir. Thank you.

**[02:11:02.610] Rep. Nelson S. Legacion** (asr-002559)  
Thank you very much.

**[02:11:03.190] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002560)  
Maraming-maraming salamat, Kong Nelson, at mabuhay po kayo.

**[02:11:06.170] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002561)  
Diyos mabalas. Thank you.

**[02:11:10.490] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-002562)  
Before we go on lunch break, I will recognize one more,

**[02:11:16.050] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-002563)  
and then we go to lunch break and toilet break as well with the Secretary.

Okay.

**[02:11:23.830] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-002565)  
The Chair now recognizes Representative Sarah Elago.

**[02:11:29.370] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002566)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Chair.

**[02:11:31.350] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002567)  
Magandang araw.

**[02:11:32.490] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002568)  
As a DepEd family, tungkol sa implementation ng Bawal Bastos Law,

**[02:11:37.470] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002569)  
ang programa at activities para rito ay kinukuha mula sa Learner Rights and Protection Funds,

**[02:11:43.630] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002570)  
pati na rin sa Gender and Development Budget.

**[02:11:46.450] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002571)

**[02:11:52.770] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002572)

**[02:12:17.200] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002573)  
Your Honor, they're still in the process of completing.

**[02:12:19.500] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002574)  
The plans that you mentioned, Your Honor, the budget is subsumed under the Gender and Development portion of the DepEd budget, Your Honor.

**[02:12:27.340] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002575)  
We can submit a report per region, Your Honor.

**[02:12:31.100] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002576)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Chair.

**[02:12:32.980] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002577)  
Abukad sa mismo report ng Bawal Bastos Law implementation,

**[02:12:37.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002578)  
nais din natin makuha kung magkano rin yung nagagastos para sa mga referral at intervention programs,

**[02:12:44.380] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002579)  
lalo na sa mental health, psychosocial support services,

**[02:12:47.940] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002580)  
para sa mga victim-survivors of sexual violence.

**[02:12:52.700] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002581)  
Hindi lang po ito para sa mga estudyante, Madam Chair.

**[02:12:55.700] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002582)  
Pero maski ho ang mga guro at ibang mga non-teaching personnel at lahat ng education stakeholders

**[02:13:01.320] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002583)  
ay kailangan nakaka-access ng ganitong mahalagang mga servisyo.

**[02:13:06.800] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002584)  
Ngayon naman, Madam Chair, nais kong magtanong tungkol sa policy ng DepEd tungkol sa class suspension.

**[02:13:13.980] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002585)  
Dahil ngayon, sunod-sunod ang bagyo.

**[02:13:16.700] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002586)  
May habagat na may dalang

**[02:13:17.940] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002587)  
malakas na pag-ulan, nandiyan ang epekto ng pagbaha,

**[02:13:22.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002588)  
nandyan din ang bantanang super el nino at extreme heat.

**[02:13:26.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002589)  
At ang risulta nito, dumarami at sunod-sunod ang bilang ng nawawalang school days dahil sa class suspension.

**[02:13:33.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002590)  
May development na huba kung paano ma-address ng DepEd

**[02:13:37.640] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002591)  
yung mga concerns ng ating kababayan tungkol sa mga late na class suspension.

**[02:13:42.460] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002592)  
May pondo bang ilalaan para sa catch-up programs, make-up classes,

**[02:13:47.940] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002593)  
paano namu-monitor at natitiyak na effective ang mga programa na ito?

**[02:13:53.700] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002594)  
Thank you, Madam Chair, Your Honor.

**[02:13:57.560] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002595)  
Meron po tayong draft EO to amend the existing EO 66.

**[02:14:04.360] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002596)  
So, in-approve na po nung SDC or Social Development Committee of DepDev.

**[02:14:11.260] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002597)  
That was last week lang.

**[02:14:12.280] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002598)  
And ang highlights nun, yung nabanggit nyo,

**[02:14:16.460] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002599)  
yung kasi dati, ano lang eh, weather hazard, rain and wind hazard lang ho.

**[02:14:23.120] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002600)  
Ngayon, kasama na ho yung earthquake.

**[02:14:25.100] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002601)  
So, may automatic suspensions for pag yung air quality is based on the limits that DNR sets,

**[02:14:32.740] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002602)  
yung earthquake, certain earthquake level, tapos may pag-asa levels for heat index din po.

**[02:14:40.660] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002603)  
So, sinama na ho natin yun.

**[02:14:42.220] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002604)  
So, yung hazards na expand po yung menu.

**[02:14:44.220] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002605)  
And we have such...

**[02:14:46.460] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002606)  
We're developing this new concept called the granular suspension and resumption of class, ma'am.

**[02:14:52.120] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002607)  
Anong ibig sabihin ito?

**[02:14:53.880] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002608)  
Kapag kasi sa ngayon, meron tayong automatic suspension sa national level or sa provincial levels.

**[02:14:58.700] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002609)  
Pagka...

**[02:15:00.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002610)  
yung certain color index ng ulan.

**[02:15:04.640] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002611)  
Tapos meron din tayong local suspension

**[02:15:06.100] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002612)  
at the level of the governor, the mayors.

**[02:15:08.960] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002613)  
So ngayon, meron ho kasing ang naging feedback ho sa amin

**[02:15:12.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002614)  
in our consultations is yung mga ibang skwelahan

**[02:15:16.380] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002615)  
na sakop doon mga suspension na nabanggit,

**[02:15:19.500] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002616)  
eh hindi naman sila lahat apektado.

**[02:15:22.040] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002617)  
Ibig sabihin, nag-declare si governor dito,

**[02:15:24.740] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002618)  
eh itong sityo na ito, ito yung barangay,

**[02:15:27.220] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002619)  
hindi pala umuulan doon sa lugar nila.

**[02:15:28.960] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002620)  
So we're under this new draft EO,

**[02:15:31.820] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002621)  
which will be

**[02:15:33.220] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002622)  
hopefully approved by the president in the coming weeks.

**[02:15:36.840] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002623)  
Pwede na pong mag-resume with coordination

**[02:15:39.840] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002624)  
with the local chief executives,

**[02:15:41.940] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002625)  
ang ating mga public and private schools, Your Honor,

**[02:15:44.500] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002626)  
para ma-minimize na nga yung learning loss.

**[02:15:48.720] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002627)  
And really, tama ho kayo because this is a very relevant topic

**[02:15:52.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002628)  
given the effects of El Nino,

**[02:15:55.360] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002629)  
the effects of climate change,

**[02:15:56.520] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002630)  
yung haze mula sa Indonesia,

**[02:15:58.400] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002631)  
talagang unprecedented po yung weather disturbances

**[02:16:01.220] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002632)  
that we're seeing at present, Your Honor.

**[02:16:04.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002633)  
Madam Chair, sa nabanggit na halimbawa ng ating Secretary,

**[02:16:08.440] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002634)  
sa kabilang banda, mayroon din mga kaso na

**[02:16:10.900] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002635)  
hindi affected yung eskwelahan,

**[02:16:14.220] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002636)  
pero yung mga estudyante sa mga lugar na binaha

**[02:16:16.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002637)  
o sa mga lugar na affected,

**[02:16:19.020] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002638)  
ay may nararanasan na pagbaha

**[02:16:21.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002639)  
at iba't iba pong kahirapan sa pagpapasok.

**[02:16:24.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002640)  
Kaya dapat may isang ala-alang rin ito.

**[02:16:27.100] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002641)  
Ngayon naman, Madam Chair,

**[02:16:28.400] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002643)  
may podo ba para sa dagdag allowance

**[02:16:30.600] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002644)  
para sa home-based work ng mga guro?

**[02:16:33.460] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002645)  
Kasi ho, diba, ang karaniwan na nangyayari

**[02:16:35.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002646)  
pag may class suspension,

**[02:16:37.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002647)  
sila ho ay nagkakandak na kanilang klase

**[02:16:41.640] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002648)  
mula sa kanilang bahay through online means.

**[02:16:44.820] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002649)  
Meron po bang dagdag na allowance

**[02:16:46.300] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002650)  
para sa home-based work na ito?

**[02:16:50.150] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002651)  
Wala po for home-based work,

**[02:16:51.650] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002652)  
pero tama ho kayo, dapat maging flexible ho tayo

**[02:16:54.110] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002653)  
dahil maaring yung eskwelahan hindi apektado,

**[02:16:56.410] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002654)  
pero yung estudyante o yung guro ay apektado

**[02:16:59.350] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002655)  
dahil nakatira sila sa ibang lugar.

**[02:17:01.810] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002656)  
So, we'll make allowance for that, Your Honor,

**[02:17:04.270] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002657)  
dun sa allowing certain students

**[02:17:07.970] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002658)  
to be excused from attending school

**[02:17:10.190] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002659)  
during those times

**[02:17:11.970] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002660)  
and using alternative delivery means

**[02:17:14.330] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002661)  
na lang or modes

**[02:17:15.170] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002662)  
dito sa kanilang mga estudyante na apektado,

**[02:17:18.790] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002663)  
Your Honor.

**[02:17:19.570] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002664)  
Madam Chair, bukod po dun sa allowance,

**[02:17:21.950] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002665)  
dagdag na allowance para sa home-based work

**[02:17:23.930] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002666)  
ng mga teachers,

**[02:17:25.790] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002667)  
nire-raise ko rin po yung concern.

**[02:17:27.050] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002668)  
Halimbawa, yung mga laptop na,

**[02:17:29.350] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002669)  
provided for teachers,

**[02:17:30.950] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002670)  
magagamit lang nila mismo dun sa school premises

**[02:17:33.350] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002671)  
at hindi po sila pinapayagan na maiuwi.

**[02:17:36.350] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002672)  
Gayun din po yung concern ng ilan na

**[02:17:38.350] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002673)  
hindi naman lahat ay makakashift

**[02:17:40.350] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002674)  
agad-agad sa online means.

**[02:17:42.370] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002675)  
Halimbawa, pag wala hong kuryente,

**[02:17:44.130] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002676)  
walang internet services,

**[02:17:45.870] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002677)  
sana po, maisang alang-alang ito ng DepEd.

**[02:17:48.270] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002678)  
Ngayon naman, tungkol, Madam Chair,

**[02:17:50.310] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002679)  
dun sa pagpapatupad ng DepEd Order No. 32,

**[02:17:55.470] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002680)  
this particular order mandates DepEd

**[02:17:57.750] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002681)  
to uphold human rights,

**[02:17:58.990] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002682)  
peace-building,

**[02:17:59.350] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002683)  
and child protection in all educational spaces,

**[02:18:02.610] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002684)  
particularly the use of school premises

**[02:18:05.030] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002685)  
by the military for civic action,

**[02:18:07.610] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002686)  
including medical and dental missions,

**[02:18:09.410] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002687)  
symposia, and other public service activities

**[02:18:11.390] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002688)  
is prohibited unless in response

**[02:18:14.090] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002689)  
to natural disasters or emergencies

**[02:18:16.030] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002690)  
where civil authorities require such assistance.

**[02:18:19.690] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002691)  
Ngayon po ba may pondo kayo

**[02:18:21.710] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002692)  
para sa pagpapatupad nito?

**[02:18:24.090] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002693)  
At meron ba kayong active na mechanism

**[02:18:26.450] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002694)  
for monitoring, evaluation, investigation?

**[02:18:29.350] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002695)  
Hanggang sa pagpapanagot

**[02:18:31.050] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002696)  
ng mga lumalabag nito?

**[02:18:33.850] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002697)  
Anong sanction pag lumabag?

**[02:18:37.410] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002698)  
Well, nirirespeto naman, by and large,

**[02:18:39.830] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002699)  
nirirespeto naman ho yung

**[02:18:41.150] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002700)  
ganong limitasyon.

**[02:18:42.770] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002701)  
Para naging tradisyon na rin ho yun

**[02:18:44.170] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002702)  
na yung mga armed services ay

**[02:18:46.750] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002703)

**[02:18:49.450] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002704)  
So, I think the exceptions is

**[02:18:51.710] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002705)

at knifing

or other security incidents,

**[02:18:58.470] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002708)  
Your Honor.

**[02:18:59.350] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002709)  
May mga reports, Madam Chair,

**[02:19:01.170] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002710)  
sa representation na ito

**[02:19:19.380] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002718)  
sa pagbabawal ho sa civilian na

**[02:19:21.780] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002719)  
lalo lalo na sa mga bata

na paghawak ng ganito

at exposure mismo sa guns.

**[02:19:27.180] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002722)  
Ngayon naman, tungkol ho sa pondo

**[02:19:29.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002723)  
para sa debt servicing

**[02:19:30.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002724)  
na mas malaki pa ho sa basic

**[02:19:32.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002725)  
education budget,

**[02:19:34.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002726)  
sabi po sa Constitution,

**[02:19:36.180] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002727)  
dapat highest budgetary priority

**[02:19:38.700] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002728)

**[02:19:41.800] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002729)  
Pero ngayon, hung 2027,

**[02:19:44.500] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002730)  
sa pangkabuan,

**[02:19:45.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002731)  
ang pondo para sa principal

**[02:19:47.360] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002732)  
amortization at debt interest

**[02:19:49.660] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002733)  
payments, umabot na ng

**[02:19:51.360] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002734)  
2.7 trillion pesos,

**[02:19:53.360] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002735)  
samantalang ang pondo po natin

**[02:19:55.280] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002736)  
sa basic education,

**[02:19:56.600] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002737)  
976 billion lamang.

**[02:19:58.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002738)  
Hindi po ba ito direktang paglabag

**[02:20:00.480] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002739)  
sa prinsipyong

**[02:20:02.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002740)  
konstitusyonal na ang

**[02:20:07.320] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002743)  
sa budget?

**[02:20:08.780] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002744)  
Paano may ipapatupad ng DepEd

**[02:20:10.540] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002745)  
ng buong buong mandato nito

**[02:20:12.340] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002746)  
lalo lalo na sa harap ng

**[02:20:13.780] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002747)

**[02:20:15.460] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002748)  
na one third lang po ng pondo

**[02:20:17.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002749)  
ng basic education

**[02:20:18.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002750)  
ay yung ginagastos po po natin

**[02:20:21.780] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002751)  
para sa utang?

**[02:20:23.480] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002752)  
Magandang tanong ho yan,

**[02:20:24.340] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002753)  
Your Honor.

**[02:20:25.120] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002754)  
But that question was already

**[02:20:26.040] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002755)  
ruled on by the Supreme Court

**[02:20:27.900] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002756)  
in the 1980s or

**[02:20:29.120] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002757)  
1990s,

**[02:20:30.600] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002758)  
in the [unclear]

**[02:20:31.440] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002759)  
[unclear].

**[02:20:32.040] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002760)  
Your Honor, and the Supreme Court

**[02:20:34.040] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002761)  
ruled that it was

**[02:20:35.920] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002762)  
alright if the automatic appropriation

**[02:20:38.200] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002763)  
for debt payments exceeded

**[02:20:40.080] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002764)  
the education budget,

**[02:20:41.960] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002765)  
Your Honor. But having said that,

**[02:20:44.040] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002766)  
ako'y nakikita ko naman, wala namang masama

**[02:20:45.740] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002767)  
kung umutang tayo. Basta gastusin ho natin

**[02:20:47.700] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002768)  
sa tama. Gastusin natin sa tao,

**[02:20:49.780] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002769)  
sa edukasyon, sa kalusugan.

**[02:20:52.060] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002770)  
Huwag mapunta sana sa katiwalian.

**[02:20:54.100] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002771)  
So, yun ho ang mahalaga.

**[02:20:56.820] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002772)  
Kung titignan ho natin

**[02:20:58.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002773)  
yung mga mauunlad at mayayaman na bansa,

**[02:20:59.940] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002774)  
malaki din naman ang utang nila. Pero siguro

**[02:21:01.800] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002775)  
mas maingat sila sa mga gastusin.

**[02:21:04.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002776)

**[02:21:06.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002777)

**[02:21:09.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002778)

**[02:21:10.620] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002779)

**[02:21:12.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002780)  
ng Kongreso na ito

**[02:21:14.180] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002781)  
ang ating automatic

**[02:21:16.120] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002782)

**[02:21:17.820] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002783)  
sa pagbabayad ng utang.

**[02:21:20.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002784)  
Gayun din,

**[02:21:22.820] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002785)  
sa ngayon kasi yung paglalaan

**[02:21:24.240] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002786)

**[02:21:26.340] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002787)

**[02:21:27.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002788)

**[02:21:31.780] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002790)

**[02:21:33.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002791)  
para sa susunod na taon.

**[02:21:35.700] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002792)  
Ibig sabihin, Madam Chair, mas mataas pa ho ito

**[02:21:37.900] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002793)

**[02:21:40.820] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002794)

**[02:21:42.260] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002795)

**[02:21:43.660] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002796)

**[02:21:45.340] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002797)

**[02:21:47.500] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002798)  
Kung seryoso ho tayo na bigyan

**[02:21:49.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002799)  
ng prioridad ng edukasyon,

**[02:21:51.580] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002800)

**[02:21:53.500] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002801)

**[02:21:55.480] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002802)  
ng attached agencies nito.

**[02:21:58.060] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002803)  
Ang dami hong budget cuts na dapat

**[02:21:59.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002804)  
naman ma-restore sa 2026,

**[02:22:01.780] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002805)  
na level ang pondo ng mga ito.

**[02:22:04.980] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002806)  
Paunla rin natin ang

**[02:22:05.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002807)

**[02:22:07.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002808)  
na may binipisyo hindi lang sa sektor

**[02:22:09.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002809)  
ng edukasyon, kundi para sa mas

**[02:22:11.660] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002810)

**[02:22:13.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002811)

**[02:22:15.840] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002812)  
kababayan. Maraming salamat, Madam Chair,

**[02:22:17.900] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002813)  
and we look forward to the

**[02:22:19.360] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002814)

**[02:22:21.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002815)  
status reports on

**[02:22:23.620] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002816)  
the said concerns, as well as

**[02:22:25.780] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002817)  
to add, Madam Chair,

with its gender and development

**[02:22:33.580] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002821)

**[02:22:38.980] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002824)  
strong support for education.

**[03:21:37.460] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-002836)  
The chair would like to recognize Representative Dadah Ismula for her question.

**[03:21:46.330] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002837)  
Assalamualaikum.

**[03:21:50.630] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002838)  
Magandang tanghali po, Madam Chair and to the DepEd family.

**[03:21:57.970] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002839)  
May mga ilang katanungan lamang po ako.

**[03:22:01.850] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002840)  
Magsisimula po ako sa Madrasa Education Program ng DepEd po.

**[03:22:06.930] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002841)  
Sa ilalim ng NEP na 2027, ang budget para sa Madrasa Education Program ay nasa 504 million pesos, which is below the 2026 level.

**[03:22:21.570] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002842)  
Magkano po kaya ang original proposal ng DepEd for the Madrasa Education Program to DBM before the NEP po?

**[03:22:31.550] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002843)  
Yes, thank you.

**[03:22:34.330] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002844)  
Magandang hapon, Madam Chair.

**[03:22:36.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002845)  
Your Honor, sa Madrasa nag-propose po ang DepEd for 2027 ng 2.03 billion, Your Honor.

**[03:22:45.170] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002846)  
2.03 billion para lamang po ito sa Madrasa Education, tama po?

**[03:22:50.810] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002847)  
Tama po, Ma'am.

**[03:22:51.410] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002848)  
Can you explain to this committee, Madam Chair, Mr. Secretary, in detail kung anong programs ang mababawas for our Moro community if hindi madadagdagan ng budget po?

**[03:23:15.740] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002849)  
This is for the...

**[03:23:17.140] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002850)  
increase in subsidy and additional asset needs.

**[03:23:21.160] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002851)  
Yung compensation po ng 670 asset needs handling the Alive classes, the Arabic language classes, Your Honor.

**[03:23:29.340] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002852)  
So, ibig sabihin po, malaki po yung matatapyas sa kanila.

**[03:23:35.000] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002853)  
Madam Chair, Mr. Secretary, sa fiscal year of 2027 allocation, ilang learners at asset needs ang kayang masuportahan?

**[03:23:45.340] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002854)  
Masuportahan.

**[03:23:47.140] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002855)  
2026 or 2027, ho?

**[03:23:48.960] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002856)  
2027, po. Allocation.

**[03:23:54.090] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002857)  
Ilan yung may plantilla items at magkano ang magiging compensation para sa mga asatids kada buwan?

**[03:24:02.710] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002858)  
Your Honor, 2,906, po.

**[03:24:09.140] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002859)  
At nasa magkano naman po kaya ang magiging compensation ng asatids kada buwan?

**[03:24:15.940] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002860)  
12,500, Your Honor.

**[03:24:19.100] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002861)  
Ito po ba ay kapareho lamang ng 2026?

**[03:24:25.720] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002862)  
Current year or mas bumaba, po?

**[03:24:29.740] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002863)  
Parehas lang, ho.

**[03:24:30.840] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002864)  
Parehas lang, po. Okay.

**[03:24:32.860] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002865)  
Madam Chair, in the proper time, Akbayan will push to increase the budget for the Madrasa Education Program.

**[03:24:39.680] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002866)  
Sa buong taong pakikipag-usap ko po sa ating Moro community sa kanilang pag-rehabilitate ng Madrasa schools,

**[03:24:47.780] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002867)  
mahalagang hindi maiiwan ang Madrasa system, which is important and integral to Muslim Filipinos.

**[03:24:55.720] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002868)  
Dadako naman po ako sa IPED.

**[03:24:58.740] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002869)  
Mula sa 154 million sa gaan ng 2026, ay bumagsak po ito sa 76 million ang pondo para sa Indigenous People's Education sa NEP ng 2027.

**[03:25:13.660] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002870)  
Madam Chair, Mr. Secretary, bakit po kaya bumaba ng higit kalahati ang item natin sa IPED?

**[03:25:21.820] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002871)  
Mula 154 million sa gaan ng 2026.

**[03:25:25.720] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002872)  
Iking 76 million na lang sa NEP ng 2027.

**[03:25:30.740] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002873)  
Kung ikukumpara po sa NEP ng 2026, 74.8 million ang NEP noong 2026.

**[03:25:38.820] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002874)  
Dinagdagan po ng Kongreso, naging 154.4 million sa GAA or General Appropriations Act for this year, Your Honor.

**[03:25:46.820] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002875)  
Kaya usually, ganoon ang practice po ng DBM.

**[03:25:49.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002876)  
Tinitingnan lang nila yung NEP level. Hindi nila ina-appropriate for the succeeding year yung GAA level.

**[03:25:55.720] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002877)  
Ang assumption nila, siguro, baka dadagdagan ulit po ng Kongreso.

**[03:26:00.880] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002878)  
So, actually, this 2027 NEP increased by a little over a million from the NEP level of 2026, Your Honor.

**[03:26:08.700] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002879)  
Madam Chair, Mr. Secretary, alin po kayang mga activities or communities or learners groups ang pinakatatamaan ng pagbawas na ito?

**[03:26:18.180] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002880)  
Kung sakaling hindi po ito maibalik sa original na requested?

**[03:26:24.600] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002881)  
Well, yung support.

**[03:26:25.720] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002882)  
Support funds for the delivery of the IPED program.

**[03:26:29.040] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002883)  
We can cover 4,471 more schools, Your Honor, pagka nabigay po yung additional funding.

**[03:26:36.200] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002884)  
Tapos yung learning and development for learning delivery, translanguaging training.

**[03:26:40.960] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002885)  
Pangalawang batch na po yun.

**[03:26:42.740] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002886)  
Tapos merong policies and standards for interagency agreements to address the access concerns of our IP learners, Your Honor.

**[03:26:49.360] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002887)  
Yun yung mga tatamaan po.

**[03:26:51.820] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002888)  
Madam Chair, Mr. Secretary, ito yung mahalaga po sa akin.

**[03:26:55.720] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002889)  
Dahil particular na ako po ay isang indigenous people at karamihan po ng aking mga kababayan, mga constituents na IP ay lumalapit patungkol po dito.

**[03:27:07.520] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002890)  
Kaya nababahala po ako kung ito po ay hindi mai-increase, Madam Chair.

**[03:27:13.240] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002891)  
Sa 76 million na allocation, ilan po kayang indigenous communities at learners lang po ang kayang masuportahan ng DEPED dito?

**[03:27:21.560] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002892)  
At ano po kaya ang critical unmet needs?

**[03:27:25.720] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002893)  
Na hindi na matutugunan sa ganitong level ng pondo po?

**[03:27:30.520] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002894)  
Ang masaserve po natin ay 560,700 learners and 4,400 schools.

**[03:27:39.140] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002895)  
Tapos pag nadagdagan po yung hinihingi ng 78 million, which is to bring it up to 2026 level, ganoon din po yung number of schools na masisilbihan, Your Honor.

**[03:27:50.660] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002896)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Chair.

**[03:27:52.140] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002897)  
Ano pong resources ang nakalaan para sa culturally?

**[03:27:55.720] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002898)  
The responsive learning materials, active community participation, paggamit ng indigenous languages, at pagpapatayo o pagpapanatili ng learning spaces para sa mga IP learners.

**[03:28:10.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002899)  
Well, that's all part of the localization of the curriculum, Your Honor.

**[03:28:15.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002900)  
Pinapayagan natin yung mga divisions at saka yung mga regions to give priority.

**[03:28:19.880] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002901)  
So makikita ko natin merong mga local traditions na pinipreserve like weaving.

**[03:28:25.720] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002902)  
Basket making and other livelihood and traditions of the IPs, Your Honor.

**[03:28:34.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002903)  
At syempre concerned din po kami sa access.

**[03:28:37.160] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002904)  
So yung pagtatayo ng additional classrooms and of course yung pagtayo ng mga integrated schools din sa ibang IP areas po ay tinutuguran po natin dahil nakikita po natin sa mga last mile schools.

**[03:28:54.220] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002905)  
Yun din po ang minsan.

**[03:28:55.720] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002906)  
Karamihan ng apektado ay IP communities din po, Madam Chair, Your Honor.

**[03:29:01.920] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002907)  
So ito yung ilang nakikita po natin.

**[03:29:05.200] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002908)  
In fact, meron kami nakakasamang IP sa Misamis noon na sabi niya dati hanggang grade 3 lang sila pero ngayon dahil nagtayuhu ng DepEd ng high school doon, first time na sa tribo nila makakagraduate sila ng high school, Your Honor.

**[03:29:20.340] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002909)  
Tama po yan, Madam Chair, Mr. Secretary.

**[03:29:22.960] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002910)  
Sa sariling ancestral domain ko po.

**[03:29:25.720] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002911)  
Madam Chair, Mr. Secretary, dati nasa grade 1, grade 2 lamang.

**[03:29:30.160] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002912)  
Ngayon may grade 6 na po kami.

**[03:29:32.420] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002913)  
And sana, insya Allah, magkaroon na rin po ito ng high school eventually.

**[03:29:37.420] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002914)  
But anyway, I will proceed to another topic po.

**[03:29:41.020] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002915)  
Ito naman po ay patungkol sa Islamic studies and Arabic language.

**[03:29:46.680] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002916)  
Teachers across Sulu.

**[03:29:49.680] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002917)  
Madam Chair, Mr. Secretary, nitong June 25 of 2026,

**[03:29:55.720] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002918)  
ni-review ng MBHTE ang contracts ng mahigit 700

**[03:29:59.920] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002935)  
Islamic Studies and Arabic Language

**[03:30:02.980] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002936)  
or ISAL teachers

**[03:30:04.800] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002937)  
sa Sulu.

**[03:30:06.180] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002938)  
At sa ilalim ng joint memorandum

**[03:30:09.240] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002939)  
na nilagdaan ng

**[03:30:10.460] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002940)  
MBHTE at DepEd

**[03:30:12.580] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002941)  
noong February 5 of

**[03:30:14.120] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002942)  
2026, ang Bangsa Moro

**[03:45:50.560] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003372)  
uh madam chair so but kadalasan kasi hindi natutuloy yung early procurement like we

**[03:45:55.600] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003373)  
did it in our first year

**[03:45:57.840] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003374)  
Pero yung ginawa namin, naiba dahil iniba po yung [unclear] from the NEP to the GAA. Nag-iba-iba yung allotments, kaya yung bid sa early procurement namin nag-iba na ho by the time after the bicam. So walang silbi din yung ginawa naming early procurement, Your Honor.

**[03:46:16.480] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003375)  
but uh definitely i think we've improved our early our our absorptive capacity with respect to

**[03:46:22.800] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003376)  
the digital uh and the computerization program because we'll be procuring

**[03:46:28.480] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003377)  
hundreds of thousands of computers this coming few months your honor

**[03:46:32.880] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-003378)  
yes i think um the question refers to the ways forward probably the actions to be taken

**[03:46:39.920] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-003379)  
by the department in order to improve the utilization

**[03:46:45.840] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003380)  
number two decentralization we're giving downloading some funds also to the regions

**[03:46:54.640] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003381)  
because we don't do if it's a

**[03:46:57.840] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003382)  
recurring procurement it means you're procuring the same items every year you don't we don't wait

**[03:47:02.560] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003383)  
to do a new policy we just have the same old policy so that suppliers can already prepare

**[03:47:07.440] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003384)  
and there are no more changes in that policy, Madam Chair.

**[03:47:12.500] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-003384-split-2)  
Thank you. Last question from the CSO

**[03:47:17.200] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-003385)  
on the learning continuity spaces debug has released its guidelines on learning continuity

**[03:47:23.920] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-003386)  
during disasters

**[03:47:25.200] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-003387)  
Part of the guidelines is the construction of these learning continuity spaces, which are aimed to be semi-permanent during disasters.

**[03:47:34.540] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-003388)  
These additional construction items are line items prone to corruption and low efficiency in building.

**[03:47:41.820] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-003389)  
How does the department plan to build and rebuild, given they are semi-permanent, these learning continuity spaces?

**[03:47:49.420] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-003390)  
And given the classroom backlogs, do we even have the capacity to build such learning continuity spaces?

**[03:47:57.500] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003391)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[03:47:59.220] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003392)  
Yung learning continuity spaces, ito?y innovation po namin in the last year.

**[03:48:05.060] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003393)  
And in the last three months, we put up 169 of these.

**[03:48:08.540] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003394)  
Kasi mabilis lang itayo ?to. Parang Lego ho siya at bakal ho siya.

**[03:48:11.940] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003395)  
Ang problema lang namin dito, minsan nawawala yung piyesa, yung tinatawag na sandwich panel, which is the...

**[03:48:19.400] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003396)  
portion which connects the classroom.

**[03:48:24.480] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003397)  
But it?s no longer the... 

**[03:48:26.260] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003398)  
Kasi dati walang standard eh.

**[03:48:27.740] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003399)  
Minsan kawayan, minsan nipa.

**[03:48:30.440] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003400)  
So ngayon, bakal na po.

**[03:48:31.980] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003401)  
And the warranty is 20 years, Your Honor.

**[03:48:34.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003402)  
So nung nakita nga nung ibang mayor, sabi,

**[03:48:37.140] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003403)  
Iyan na lang ibigay n?yo sa amin. Huwag na...

**[03:48:38.680] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003404)  
And regarding the corruption, we are getting good value, I think,

**[03:48:42.540] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003405)  
because these are being built for under 2 million, Your Honor.

**[03:48:45.920] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003406)  
And they last for a minimum of 20 years.

**[03:48:49.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003407)  
Possibly more, Your Honor.

**[03:48:52.420] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-003408)  
Thank you.

**[03:48:55.470] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-003409)  
Thank you for answering all the questions raised by the CSO.

**[03:49:02.170] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003410)  
You're most welcome, Madam Chair.

**[03:49:03.150] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003411)  
And to the committee.

**[03:49:05.590] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-003412)  
Now, let us proceed to the other members.

**[03:49:09.850] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-003413)  
The Chair would like to recognize Representative Presley De Jesus for his question.

**[03:49:18.310] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003414)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[03:49:19.930] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003416)  
Of course, to our...

**[03:49:21.350] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003417)  
DepEd family, headed by our Secretary,

**[03:49:25.210] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003418)  
boss, good afternoon sa inyong lahat.

**[03:49:27.890] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003419)  
Alam niyo, kapag tinitingnan ko yung budget natin,

**[03:49:31.650] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003420)  
eh dapat talaga hindi lang kayo.

**[03:49:34.410] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003421)  
How much is our automatic appropriation?

**[03:49:36.550] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003422)  
Ilang percentage po ng total budget tayo?

**[03:49:47.330] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003423)  
2 billion po. That is for the RLIP, yung

**[03:49:49.870] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003424)  
retirement, karamihan pensions and premiums ho yan.

**[03:49:54.070] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003425)  
When you submitted your budget, Secretary,

**[03:49:56.610] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003426)  
na-approve naman lahat ba o binawasan ba o ano?

**[03:50:00.350] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003427)  
Usually binabawasan ng DBM yan, eh.

**[03:50:03.690] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003428)  
More or less intact ho siya, Your Honor.

**[03:50:05.970] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003429)  
Konti lang nabawas.

**[03:50:07.170] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003430)  
Yeah.

**[03:50:07.750] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003431)  
Kasi for me, Mr. Chair, ah, Madam Chair, ang education talaga yan ang number one.

**[03:50:13.430] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003432)  
We keep on saying America is number one.

**[03:50:15.690] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003433)  
When you look at the data of the Americans,

**[03:50:18.230] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003434)  
number seven lang sila sa lahat ng average category.

**[03:50:23.710] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003435)  
So, we...

**[03:50:25.490] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003436)  
Mr. Chair...

**[03:50:26.610] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003437)  
Madam Chair, Secretary, sa Southeast Asia,

**[03:50:29.870] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003438)  
pang-ilan tayo? At par ba tayo when it comes to public schools

**[03:50:32.990] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003439)  
in other...

**[03:50:33.710] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003440)  
sa ating karatig bansa?

**[03:50:36.390] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003441)  
Just ballpark figure.

**[03:50:39.970] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003442)  
Well, siguro sa Asia,

**[03:50:44.190] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003443)  
of course, Singapore is ahead of us.

**[03:50:47.270] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003444)  
Thailand, Malaysia are ahead of us.

**[03:50:49.390] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003445)  
Siguro ang Vietnam, nauunahan na tayo.

**[03:50:51.950] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003446)  
Siguro ang kasabay natin

**[03:50:52.890] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003447)  
Indonesia, Cambodia.

**[03:50:54.790] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003448)  
Yeah.

**[03:50:56.050] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003449)  
Nasa ilalim naman natin

**[03:50:56.590] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003450)  
yung Laos and Myanmar, Your Honor.

**[03:50:59.950] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003451)  
Yeah.

**[03:51:00.470] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003452)  
So, in short, we are...

**[03:51:02.550] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003453)  
if not on top, at least at par in the middle.

**[03:51:05.770] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003454)  
We're in the middle, but hopefully we can...

**[03:51:08.390] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003455)  
I think we have the potential to do better, Your Honor.

**[03:51:11.230] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003456)  
Yeah.

**[03:51:11.770] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003457)  
Madam Chair, Secretary, what do you think, you know,

**[03:51:15.290] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003458)  
on the onset, what do you think,

**[03:51:16.810] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003459)  
at least we have...

**[03:51:18.230] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003460)  
We cannot beat Singapore.

**[03:51:19.530] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003461)  
We know that.

**[03:51:20.390] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003462)  
It's a given.

**[03:51:21.930] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003463)  
But at least, even if we're in the middle,

**[03:51:24.330] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003464)  
what do you think we need to do?

**[03:51:26.590] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003465)  
As a nation, no?

**[03:51:28.230] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003466)  
Hindi naman lahat kayang gawin ng DepEd.

**[03:51:32.350] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003467)  
It is a whole-nation approach.

**[03:51:34.310] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003468)  
What do you think we need to do as a nation?

**[03:51:37.130] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003469)  
Ang tinitingnan ko ho yung Vietnam.

**[03:51:38.770] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003470)  
Kasi dati nauunahan ho natin ang Vietnam,

**[03:51:42.050] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003471)  
pero ngayon nalampasan na ho tayo, especially sa test scores.

**[03:51:45.190] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003472)  
They focus on certain points.

**[03:51:49.410] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003473)  
Number one is the budgetary allocation.

**[03:51:51.370] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003474)  
They spend 20% of their national budget for education.

**[03:51:54.710] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003475)  
And they...

**[03:51:56.590] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003476)  
They pay a particular focus on teachers and teacher training, Your Honor.

**[03:52:00.310] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003477)  
So they make sure that their teachers are satisfied,

**[03:52:09.630] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003478)  
at may magandang career path.

**[03:52:11.510] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003479)  
So talagang promotion, further studies ng teachers nila.

**[03:52:14.530] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003480)  
Inaalagaan talaga nila.

**[03:52:16.350] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003481)  
They focus on strengthening their teachers.

**[03:52:19.010] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003482)  
Kasi alam nila na yan ang heart, as our President said,

**[03:52:24.390] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003483)  
of the educational system.

**[03:52:26.030] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003484)  
So, I think...

**[03:52:26.590] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003485)  
So, I think those are two strong points that we can borrow from Vietnam.

**[03:52:32.310] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003486)  
Sa Indonesia, meron din silang app para sa teachers.

**[03:52:35.750] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003487)  
So may feedback mechanism with teachers.

**[03:52:38.710] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003488)  
Of course, Singapore, Thailand, Malaysia, they invest a lot in education, Your Honor,

**[03:52:46.590] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003489)  
especially early child care, Your Honor.

**[03:52:49.830] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003490)  
Thank you, Madam Chair, of course, to the Secretary.

**[03:52:52.550] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003491)  
You mentioned that in Vietnam, usually Vietnam.

**[03:52:55.430] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003492)  
Yes.

**[03:52:56.430] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003493)  
Noong early seventies, eighties, pumupunta sila sa atin para pag-aralan ang farming natin, di ba?

**[03:53:02.850] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003494)  
Ngayon, baligtad na.

**[03:53:04.030] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003495)  
Mas marami na silang napo-produce kaysa sa atin.

**[03:53:05.470] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003496)  
And then, when it comes to education, 20% of the total national budget.

**[03:53:12.110] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003497)  
So, right now, we have something like 7.2 trillion.

**[03:53:15.910] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003498)  
So I think it is only right na yung 20% to achieve.

**[03:53:20.030] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003499)  
Remember, laging sinasabi ng mga bayani natin,

**[03:53:24.190] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003500)  
kabataan ang pag-asa ng kinabukasan natin.

**[03:53:27.550] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003506)  
So I think this is the high time po na 20% is not bad.

**[03:53:31.810] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003507)  
Right now, ilang percent po ang pie natin sa budget natin?

**[03:53:38.190] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003508)  
Ilang percent?

**[03:53:39.250] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003509)  
It's about 14% of the, 14.3% of the national budget.

**[03:53:44.030] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003510)  
That's the budget.

**[03:53:45.490] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003511)  
Yeah.

**[03:53:45.910] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003512)  
So ang baba, no?

**[03:53:47.350] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003513)  
Hindi naman mababa yun.

**[03:53:48.530] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003514)  
Medyo maganda na 'yan kumpara sa dati, Your Honor.

**[03:53:50.750] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003515)  
Pero I think we should be conscious about moving it towards a certain goal.

**[03:53:54.730] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003516)  
Yeah.

**[03:53:55.390] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003517)  
You're being modest.

**[03:53:56.430] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003518)  
Secretary. Pero actually, no?

**[03:53:59.010] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003519)  
Kasi when you look at Vietnam, nakikita ko,

**[03:54:01.800] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003520)  
of course, Japan, Korea, malalayo na 'yan. Mas marami na rin silang computer, eh.

**[03:54:06.100] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003521)  
In our ratio po natin sa ating mga schools,

**[03:54:10.950] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003522)  
per school, per ano, hindi na per student, per school class na lang po.

**[03:54:16.200] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003523)  
Ilan po ang?are we behind when it comes to computers po para sa learning ng ating mga estudyante?

**[03:54:22.150] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003524)  
Medyo ho nahuli ho tayo diyan.

**[03:54:25.400] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003526)  
But the goal is really to have at least one computer lab in each school, Your Honor, para at least may optimum na setup.

**[03:54:32.110] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003527)  
there's such a thing as too much computer time also actually that's a new finding now

**[03:54:36.190] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003528)  
That is why many countries, like the Nordic countries, bumabalik na po sa pen and paper actually, because dati one gadget, one learner sila, but hindi na po iyon ang standard ngayon.

**[03:54:45.070] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003529)  
they're limiting the computer used to one to three hours a week

**[03:54:48.270] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003530)  
so that is attainable in my opinion uh

**[03:54:50.430] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003531)  
sa atin. Basta consistent tayo sa purchasing of our computers for the computer labs. And yun po ang importante po, dahil sa PISA, if you read the PISA report of 2018 and 2022,

**[03:55:05.310] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003532)  
many the feedback was that many of our learners were not familiar with digital technology

**[03:55:10.350] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003533)  
And it was the first time that they encountered using a mouse during the exam itself. Kaya itong nahuling PISA exam noong 2025, sinigurado natin lahat na kukuha ng

**[03:55:20.430] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003534)  
exam, at least makaramdam na gumamit ng computer. Hindi sila manibago dahil sayang yung oras nila pag doon nila kakabisaduhin yung paggamit ng computer.

**[03:55:30.990] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003535)  
Madam Chair, thank you, Madam Chair. So yun nga, tama si Secretary na

**[03:55:37.470] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003536)  
at least one computer per classroom. I hope it is going to be achievable.

**[03:55:44.110] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003537)  
Pagdating sa internet

**[03:55:44.430] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003538)  
connectivity natin, ano naman po ang ratio natin?

**[03:55:51.900] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003539)  
Mga 19% lang ho ang schools?99% tayo may internet. 99 pala, sorry, 99%. Pero yung importanteng point diyan is yung ibang mababa po ang bandwidth.

**[03:56:03.820] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003540)  
so we're trying to get to at least uh so there are 460 schools without internet

**[03:56:13.970] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003541)  
Having said that, yung quality ng internet is variable.

**[03:56:18.210] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003542)  
Sa iba, sa opisina lang ng principal may internet. So I guess the next goal is to aim for greater bandwidth in schools, so that you are able to

**[03:56:24.130] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003543)  
use

**[03:56:24.690] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003544)  
things like academy uh and other education software your honor

**[03:56:30.530] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003545)  
Thank you, Madam Chair. Secretary, I do not know if someone asked this question:

**[03:56:35.970] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003546)  
how do we integrate AI ngayon?

**[03:56:41.410] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003547)  
Kasi mukhang sabi nga ni Elon [unclear], in the next five years daw,

**[03:56:44.210] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003548)  
human robotics will be more intelligent than humans.

**[03:56:48.370] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003549)  
Paano tayo mag-cope up in the next few years when it comes to AI?

**[03:56:54.210] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003550)  
May upside and downside po ang AI. So I think on the upside it can really help our teachers upskill,

**[03:56:59.810] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003551)  
especially when EDCOM has reported a skills mismatch, meaning yung nagtuturo ng science

**[03:57:04.850] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003552)  
hindi pala science teacher, yung nagtuturo ng chemistry

**[03:57:09.490] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003553)  
hindi naman pala expert sa chemistry. So yung mga ganon, I think AI can really help assist, and also yung personalized learning,

**[03:57:17.250] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003554)  
each learner can proceed at their own speed. So iyon ang upside ng AI. The downside is that there are

**[03:57:21.970] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003555)  
studies which show that uh

**[03:57:23.570] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003556)  
ai can in very young children or in younger children ai can diminish the creative thinking and

**[03:57:30.930] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003557)  
the critical faculties of our learners. So iyon ang babalansehin ho natin. You have to introduce it at the right age and

**[03:57:39.650] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003558)  
with the right safeguards uh and of course we must train our teachers to use it appropriately your

**[03:57:44.850] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003559)  
Your Honor.

**[04:13:51.420] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003817)

**[04:19:04.080] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003877-split-3)  
Now, with regards

**[04:19:05.590] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003878)  
naman doon sa ating senior high school

**[04:19:11.070] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003879)  
voucher program,

**[04:19:12.690] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003880)  
I think nagkaroon tayo ng problema dito with regards to yung mga ghost beneficiaries. Ano yung ginawa nating bagong control, safeguard, so that hindi na maulit ito?

**[04:19:33.160] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003880-split-2)  
Your Honor, there were admittedly not too many safeguards before, so now we have instituted

**[04:19:40.480] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003881)  
many safeguards at various levels so at the school level

**[04:19:44.680] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003882)  
at the level of PEAC, which is the council that grants the vouchers, which DepEd chairs.

**[04:19:52.180] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003883)  
so there's five layers of validation your honor number one there's validation via the

**[04:19:56.980] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003884)  
learner information system or lis through the application programming interface so this

**[04:20:02.940] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003885)  
cross checks the the information or the lis of depth and confirm that learners are actually

**[04:20:07.900] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003886)  
enrolled in the school

**[04:20:09.320] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003887)  
Pangalawa, yung central office validation. This is a second layer of validation which reviews billing statements

**[04:20:14.680] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003888)  
at the central office level to identify discrepancies with the LIS.

**[04:20:16.980] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003889)  
So payments are not made until issues are resolved. Dati kasi bayad kaagad si DepEd. Number three, we have post-audit monitoring by PEAC, which administers the voucher system. PEAC conducts post-audit visits to participating private schools to check compliance with guidelines and accuracy of submitted data. Naging masigasig na rin po dito ang PEAC

**[04:20:42.680] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003891)  
because ayaw nilang?

**[04:20:44.520] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003892)  
the violators are not the traditional schools but some of these new schools which sprouted out

**[04:20:49.320] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003893)  
in the wake of K-to-12 reform para lang makapag-provide ng senior high school

**[04:20:55.960] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003894)  
pero maraming ghost beneficiaries, Your Honor. Pang-apat po, yung regional offices and school division offices also conduct on-site

**[04:21:02.200] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003895)  
inspections and submit reports for further validation. At panghuli, end-of-school-year PEAC

**[04:21:07.720] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003896)  
validation, where PEAC conducts a final validation at the end of the school year

**[04:21:11.960] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003897)  
to confirm the learner is still enrolled and up-to-date yung kanyang enrollment status.

**[04:21:21.640] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003898)  
At siguro idagdag ko na rin po yung fact na nag-file tayo ng maraming kaso, and we have secured one conviction. One conviction from the RTC, and we have four cases filed, Your Honor.

**[04:21:30.840] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003899)  
So I think there will be a deterrent effect, kasi

**[04:21:39.880] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003900)  
dati matatapang po yung mga eskwelahan at talagang nagbi-build sila ng mga fictitious. But now I do not think people will

**[04:21:41.960] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003901)  
think twice before doing this modus operandi.

**[04:21:44.300] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003901-split-2)  
So, Sec, it would be safe to say that with

**[04:21:46.520] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003902)  
the implementation of these new regulations and safeguards, hindi na? we would be able to minimize,

**[04:21:53.080] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003903)  
mahirap naman sabihin na totally it will be eradicated, but it will drastically minimize the occurrence

**[04:21:57.640] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003904)  
of a repeat of that problem.

**[04:22:01.440] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003904-split-2)  
Tama po, Your Honor.

**[04:22:02.480] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003904-split-3)  
And then lastly,

**[04:22:04.680] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003905)  
uh of course it's one thing if we want to invest in education it's one thing to construct

**[04:22:10.360] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003906)  
school buildings

**[04:22:11.960] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003907)  
classrooms it's another thing

kailangan equally mag-invest rin tayo sa

**[04:22:17.960] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003910)  
ng mga teachers naman natin to improve.

**[04:22:19.960] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003911)  
in fact if we compare

**[04:22:22.840] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003912)  
kagaya sa

**[04:22:23.960] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003913)  
China, last September

**[04:22:25.960] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003914)  
meron silang ni-launch na AI Plus Education program.

**[04:22:29.240] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003915)  
Naging mandatory yung

**[04:22:31.400] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003916)

so what percentage of the budget would be utilized to improve the skills of the teachers

that's to train that's the body which trains our uh our teachers in depth ed your honor so

that one will really need to modernize what we teach them and

**[04:27:32.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003976)

**[04:42:17.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004163)  
Mr. Chair DPED now accommodates APDS loans up to seven years

**[04:42:24.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004164)  
but the Magna Carta prohibits salary deductions

**[04:42:28.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004165)  
except under authority of law

**[04:42:31.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004166)  
while the GAA authority for authorized deductions is enacted annually

**[04:42:36.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004167)  
so my question is what is DPED's specific legal basis

**[04:42:41.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004168)  
for accommodating automatic payroll deductions?

**[04:42:44.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004169)  
for seven years

**[04:42:48.890] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004170)  
and this is not just a legal issue Madam Chair Mr. Secretary

**[04:42:52.890] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004171)  
documented na rin ang problema ng teachers na may multiple loans

**[04:42:56.890] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004172)  
sa ATM at halos wala nang nauuwing sweldo dahil lubog na po sa utang.

**[04:43:02.890] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004173)  
before extending the loan period to seven years

**[04:43:07.900] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004174)  
did DPED study whether this helps teachers

**[04:43:10.900] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004175)  
or simply keeps them indebted longer

**[04:43:13.900] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004176)  
how many teachers today have multiple APDS loans

**[04:43:17.900] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004177)  
and what is their actual average and median take home pay after deductions?

**[04:43:22.900] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004178)  
meron po ba tayong data?

**[04:43:24.900] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004179)  
your honor yung we're still compliant with the GAA provision

**[04:43:29.900] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004180)  
which requires that the 5,000 net must take home pay of the teacher

**[04:43:36.900] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004181)  
cannot be touched by financial institutions

**[04:43:40.900] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004182)  
and yun po ang clamor ng ating mga teachers for a longer payment period

**[04:43:45.900] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004183)  
and lower interest of course

**[04:43:48.900] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004184)  
so I think yung mga when we consulted teachers and financial institutions

**[04:43:54.900] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004185)  
they said they're limited by the BSP regulations on this

**[04:43:58.900] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004186)  
so sumulat kami sa Banko Sentral ng Pilipinas or BSP

**[04:44:02.900] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004187)  
para humingi ng opinion at kung pwedeng i-extend po sa seven years

**[04:44:07.900] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004188)  
as requested by our teachers

**[04:44:09.900] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004189)  
because many of our teachers stay in the department for a long time

**[04:44:18.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004190)  
and they're willing to have these longer loans

**[04:44:24.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004191)  
your honor

**[04:44:25.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004192)  
so pumayag naman ho

**[04:44:27.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004193)  
sa mabuting palad

**[04:44:29.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004194)  
pumayag naman ho ang BSP

**[04:44:32.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004195)  
so now we are enforcing or

**[04:44:36.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004196)  
it's just an option

**[04:44:38.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004197)  
there's no compulsion on the part of the teacher to enter into a seven year loan

**[04:44:42.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004198)  
it's an option that they

**[04:44:43.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004199)  
it's like an item on a menu

**[04:44:44.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004200)  
they can order it if they wish

**[04:44:46.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004201)  
if they don't wish to enter

**[04:44:48.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004202)  
to engage in a seven or to

**[04:44:50.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004203)  
incur a seven year obligation

**[04:44:52.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004204)  
there is no compulsion on them to incur it your honor

**[04:44:55.240] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004205)  
so if magiging applicable sa kanilang seven years

**[04:45:00.560] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004206)  
they have to sign an agreement?

**[04:45:02.700] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004207)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[04:45:03.300] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004208)  
It's purely voluntary on their part.

**[04:45:05.800] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004209)  
Yes.

**[04:45:06.980] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004210)  
We are also receiving information

**[04:45:09.100] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004211)  
that some teachers are paying around 3.5%

**[04:45:12.320] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004212)  
on interest per month

**[04:45:13.820] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004213)  
or roughly 42% per year

**[04:45:16.260] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004214)  
if simply annualized.

**[04:45:18.420] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004215)  
Is that correct?

**[04:45:19.220] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004216)  
And what is the highest interest rate

**[04:45:21.640] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004217)

**[04:45:25.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004218)  
And does DEPED impose any ceiling

**[04:45:28.040] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004219)

**[04:45:32.480] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004221)

**[04:45:35.420] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004222)

**[04:45:37.360] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004223)

**[04:45:42.060] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004224)

**[04:45:47.860] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004225)

**[04:45:49.760] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004226)  
But again, it's their choice.

**[04:45:51.360] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004227)

**[04:45:56.340] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004228)

**[04:45:58.860] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004229)

**[04:46:00.540] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004230)  
So if they have 5,000,

**[04:46:01.600] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004231)  
we're asking the financial institutions

**[04:46:03.220] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004232)

**[04:46:05.420] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004233)  
We're showing them an altered document

**[04:46:07.380] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004234)  
to show that they still have 5,000.

**[04:46:12.440] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004235)  
Pero does the BSP allow these institutions

**[04:46:19.840] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004236)

**[04:46:22.260] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004237)

**[04:46:26.320] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004238)  
Mga loan sharks na ho iyan.

**[04:46:28.320] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004239)  
Pero mayroon na ho kayong mga nahuli na nag-charge ng mas mataas?

**[04:46:31.600] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004240)

**[04:46:33.780] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004241)

**[04:46:36.040] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004242)

**[04:46:37.060] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004243)  
Or basically, ni-report ninyo po?

**[04:46:38.640] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004244)

**[04:46:44.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004245)

**[04:46:48.600] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004246)  
Baka walang lisensya ho iyan.

**[04:46:50.180] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004247)  
Suspetso ko walang lisensya ho iyan.

**[04:46:52.480] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004248)  
Thank you.

**[05:16:37.230] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-004687)
