**[05:16:55.250] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-004688)  
na sana po ay magkaroon ng talagang inter-agency task force na talaga pong mag-ayos nito.

**[05:17:09.490] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-004689)  
Mr. Che, yun po lang.

**[05:17:14.300] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004690)  
Thank you, Your Honor, Congressman Jay, Madam Chair.

**[05:17:19.120] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004691)  
I think you're right, and definitely, in fact, tomorrow we have a scheduled meeting with DICTE, CHED, and other agencies.

**[05:17:26.880] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004692)  
To talk about the online aspect of the violence, Your Honor, kasi bahagi rin yun.

**[05:17:31.840] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004693)  
Kung anong nakikita ng mga bata online, minsan yun ang gusto nilang mangyari sa labas ng silidaraan o sa loob at labas.

**[05:17:40.080] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004694)  
So, definitely, we're most willing to coordinate kasi this incidence of school shootings, maraming aspeto yun eh.

**[05:17:51.060] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004695)  
Yung security aspect ng school, yung mental health ng ating learners,

**[05:17:54.820] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004696)  
yung ability of our teachers to connect the sense of community within a school, yung gun control measures po natin.

**[05:18:06.320] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004697)  
Kung baga tahi-tahi na po itong mga issue natin.

**[05:18:08.860] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004698)  
So, we thank you for your concern, sir.

**[05:18:11.400] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-004699)  
So, gusto ko lamang po sana na ito po ay inampisaan ng aking asawa na si former congressman,

**[05:18:24.820] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-004700)  
Congresswoman Vergara.

**[05:18:27.700] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-004701)  
Ito po ay taong dalawang libo't labimpito.

**[05:18:33.020] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-004702)  
Dahil po hindi ho pa nariresolba pa namin, kaya ganoon ang naging aking mungkahi.

**[05:18:44.800] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-004703)  
So, hayaan lamang po niyong ipakita ko yung salita ng aking asawa noong 2017.

**[05:18:54.820] Rep. Rosanna "Ria" V. Vergara** (asr-004704)  
The internet is a double-edged sword, a tool for convenience which anyone with Wi-Fi access or a data plan may use, and if done so improperly, abuse.

**[05:19:10.400] Presentation video** (asr-004705)  
Noong May 15, 2017, naghatid ng isang privileged speech si Congresswoman Vergara upang magbigay babala sa mga panganib na dulot ng internet, lalo na sa mga kabataan.

**[05:19:23.520] Presentation video** (asr-004706)  
Isa sa mga sinusulong na batas ni Congresswoman Rosana Ria Vergara

**[05:19:26.940] Presentation video** (asr-004707)  
ay ang House Bill 5666, o an act to integrate internet and social media education as part of the curriculum of all primary and secondary schools, public and private.

**[05:19:38.820] Presentation video** (asr-004708)  
Ang pangunahing layuni ng panukalang batas na ito ay bumuo ng isang pangasiwaan na magbabantay at magtuturo sa mga mag-aaral ng wasto at maingat na paggamit ng internet.

**[05:19:49.540] Rep. Rosanna "Ria" V. Vergara** (asr-004709)  
I will file a House Bill that will require the Department of Education, a subject that will specifically deal with the internet.

**[05:19:56.940] Rep. Rosanna "Ria" V. Vergara** (asr-004710)  
and social media awareness, and the dangers and risks posed to the young by internet access that include the danger of internet gaming.

**[05:20:07.380] Presentation video** (asr-004711)  
Ilan sa mga panganib na kanyang tinukoy ay ang pagsusugal at adiksyon sa mga internet games.

**[05:20:13.680] Rep. Rosanna "Ria" V. Vergara** (asr-004712)  
How can a game be the reason for skipping class and losing money?

**[05:20:19.080] Rep. Rosanna "Ria" V. Vergara** (asr-004713)  
Because it involves gambling.

**[05:20:21.940] Rep. Rosanna "Ria" V. Vergara** (asr-004714)  
And because it is designed by the developers to be addictive.

**[05:20:25.600] Presentation video** (asr-004715)  
Kabilang din dito ang Blue Whale Challenge na nag-uudyok sa mga sumasali dito na saktan ang sarili at ang pinakamalala ay udyukan silang magpakamatay.

**[05:20:35.680] Rep. Rosanna "Ria" V. Vergara** (asr-004716)  
Recently, a new social media game called Blue Whale was developed.

**[05:20:41.940] Rep. Rosanna "Ria" V. Vergara** (asr-004717)  
The players are tasked to do 50 strange things.

**[05:20:46.540] Rep. Rosanna "Ria" V. Vergara** (asr-004718)  
Hurting themselves, waking up at early hours to watch horror films, to win the game, commit suicide.

**[05:20:55.600] Presentation video** (asr-004719)  
Kamakailan lamang, may isa na namang internet app na may kahalintulad na tema, ang Momo Challenge.

**[05:21:01.440] Presentation video** (asr-004720)  
Nanawagan naman ngayon ang Philippine National Police sa mga magulang na bantayang mabuti ang kanilang mga anak sa paggamit ng internet.

**[05:21:09.420] Presentation video** (asr-004721)  
Matapos ito sa nababalitang issue sa online na tinatawag na Momo Challenge.

**[05:21:15.020] Presentation video** (asr-004722)  
Ang challenge ay naghihikayat sa mga bata na gumawa ng mga task kasama na rito ang pananakit sa sarili at pagpapakamatay.

**[05:21:23.340] Presentation video** (asr-004723)  
Totoo man o hindi?

**[05:21:25.040] Presentation video** (asr-004724)  
Ang panganib.

**[05:21:25.600] Presentation video** (asr-004725)  
Ang panganib na dala sa mga menor de edad ng computer gaming at internet ay mananatiling na riyan kung hindi natin mababantayan at gagabayan ang mga kabataan.

**[05:21:35.840] Rep. Rosanna "Ria" V. Vergara** (asr-004726)  
As legislators, concerned parents, we must be vigilant that there is another kind of war going on.

**[05:21:43.280] Rep. Rosanna "Ria" V. Vergara** (asr-004727)  
In this war, it is the minds, hearts, and lives of our children, the youth, the most vulnerable individuals in our society that are being targeted.

**[05:21:54.540] Rep. Rosanna "Ria" V. Vergara** (asr-004728)  
We must be vigilant.

**[05:21:55.040] Rep. Rosanna "Ria" V. Vergara** (asr-004729)  
We must do everything to protect our children from the dangers of internet and social media gaming.

**[05:22:01.700] Rep. Rosanna "Ria" V. Vergara** (asr-004730)  
We must respond immediately for the future of an entire generation is at stake.

**[05:22:07.840] Presentation video** (asr-004731)  
Isang lingkod bayan na nakatanaw sa...

**[05:22:14.710] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-004732)  
Taon-taon po ay aming ipina-file 'yan, kaya po naging suggestion ko na hangga't wala po, sana magkaroon ng interagency task force.

**[05:22:29.230] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-004733)  
Other than that, I manifest my support, full support, for the budget of the Department of Education.

**[05:22:40.270] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-004734)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[05:22:42.030] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004735)  
Maraming salamat po, Kong J. Vergara.

**[05:22:44.890] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004736)  
Salamat.

**[05:22:45.730] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004737)  
At we support the advocacy of Kong Ria and yourself.

**[05:22:50.470] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004738)  
Dapat mapulis po talaga natin yung use of social media by our youth.

**[05:22:55.590] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004739)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[05:22:57.250] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-004740)  
Thank you very much.

**[05:22:58.410] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-004741)  
Next question is Representative Caroline Agiao.

**[05:23:06.340] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004742)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[05:23:08.380] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004743)  
Good afternoon.

**[05:23:08.940] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004744)  
Good afternoon to the DepEd family.

**[05:23:14.360] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004745)  
Regarding po dun sa...

**[05:23:16.680] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004746)  
Mr. Chair.

**[05:23:17.860] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004747)  
Ay, Madam Chair.

**[05:23:19.100] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004748)  
Regarding sa ating yung Department of Indigenous Peoples Education, or the IPED,

**[05:23:27.440] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004749)  
we recognize that indigenous peoples have the right to an education that is accessible.

**[05:23:32.980] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004750)  
And then my question is that...

**[05:23:35.100] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004751)  
DepEd, actually investing in this program, but then, ang katanungan ko po, doon sa ating special needs, for the special needs education,

**[05:23:51.680] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004752)  
can a DepEd increase the funding for the assessment of children with disabilities and for the procurement of the assistive learning devices and technologies?

**[05:24:03.560] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004753)  
How many learners?

**[05:24:04.900] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004754)  
How many learners have currently waiting for assessment?

**[05:24:09.380] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004755)  
And how many assessed learners still do not have the assistive devices?

**[05:24:14.680] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004756)  
Sa ngayon po.

**[05:24:16.820] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004757)  
For the indigenous peoples education for the IPED, because sa aming distrito po,

**[05:24:22.280] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004758)  
parang doon sa SDS po namin is they're going to include as a curriculum sa IPED,

**[05:24:30.180] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004759)  
this indigenous peoples education.

**[05:24:32.900] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004760)  
Can we substantially increase din po, and then

**[05:24:37.420] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004785)  
mag-stand alone po siya, kasi included siya doon sa isang program po ninyo, as isang, isa lang po siya na, how do you call this, nakasali siya dito sa, ano ba yan?

**[05:24:54.860] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004786)  
Anong program?

**[05:24:57.060] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004787)  
This one.

**[05:24:57.980] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004788)  
Inclusive education.

**[05:24:59.480] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004789)  
So 983.8 million.

**[05:25:03.660] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004790)  
Ito po yung budget.

**[05:25:04.780] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004791)  
But then, nandito na rin kasi, nandaloob na rin ang special needs, madrasah education, indigenous peoples education, and the multi-grade program.

**[05:25:18.200] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004792)  
So, and alternative learning system.

**[05:25:22.560] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004793)  
Can we increase also budget para dito po, Madam Chair, to our Secretary?

**[05:25:31.520] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004794)  
Thank you, Your Honor, Madam Chair.

**[05:25:34.140] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004795)  
Yung IP education or indigenous people's education, meron ng sariling line item ho yan.

**[05:25:40.040] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004796)  
Ganun din ho yung special needs education.

**[05:25:43.260] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004797)  
At nag-request ho kami ng 154 million for IPED, pero ang nag-grant lang ho is 76 million.

**[05:25:49.820] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004798)  
That's why part of our appeal is the additional 78 million for IP education.

**[05:25:54.880] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004799)  
Tapos sa SNED naman ho, ang nirequest ho namin is 1.39 billion, at ang nag-grant ho is 849.9 million.

**[05:26:02.860] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004800)  
Kaya meron tayong pending appeal for 541 million additional budget for this SNED special needs education, Your Honor.

**[05:26:13.280] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004801)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[05:26:15.320] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004802)  
Kasi itong special needs, we really need mag-additional sana po tayo ng budget for this program.

**[05:26:23.040] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004803)  
Kasi ang daming ho nating mga kabataan na they really need this additional funding for this program po.

**[05:26:31.440] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004804)  
Especially also...

**[05:26:32.860] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004805)  
So, mga indigenous po na may special needs.

**[05:26:37.120] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004806)  
And then my second question, I would also...

**[05:26:42.700] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004807)  
Regarding po dun sa katanungan ni Sir, ni Congressman Rufus kanina,

**[05:26:48.680] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004808)  
regarding the allocation, yung bidding for the school buildings for 2026,

**[05:26:57.200] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004809)  
tapos may mga FLR din for the 2025 na hindi na rin...

**[05:27:02.860] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004810)  
Lumabas under 2026.

**[05:27:05.100] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004811)  
And then ngayon sa 2027 proposed NEP, wala din yung mga school buildings na yung mga na-wash out during the typhoon.

**[05:27:15.440] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004812)  
And then may katanungan din po ako, the computation approximately 2.5 million per school building po ba?

**[05:27:22.740] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004813)  
Sa isang building, even in the mountainous area.

**[05:27:28.140] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004814)  
Kasi may mga areas na malalayo yung mga...

**[05:27:32.860] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004815)  
Lugar na walang road, walang access to roads.

**[05:27:37.160] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004816)  
And then meron din mga GEDA areas.

**[05:27:41.280] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004817)  
Hindi naman sila kasali din sa last mile.

**[05:27:44.480] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004818)  
Though it's a regular fund.

**[05:27:47.440] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004819)  
May possibility ho ba, Madam Chair, na yung costing mechanism for the school buildings located sa mga GEDA areas,

**[05:27:56.960] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004820)  
lalo na yung mga wala pang roads going to that school.

**[05:28:02.860] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004821)  
Na gagawin.

**[05:28:04.620] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004822)  
Because yung costing po nila, mag-a-add pa ng manual hauling, multiple handling.

**[05:28:13.740] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004823)  
Ganun po, Madam Chair.

**[05:28:15.820] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004824)  
So, ang katanungan ko po, Madam Chair, kung kay sa ating DepEd family,

**[05:28:23.020] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004825)  
meron ho ba talaga bang uniform the whole Philippines, even you are in the island,

**[05:28:28.160] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004826)  
nasa mountainous area, panawalang daan.

**[05:28:31.740] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004827)  
Same ho ba?

**[05:28:32.860] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004828)  
Madam Chair, na 2.5 per classroom.

**[05:28:36.020] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004829)  
Madam Chair.

**[05:28:36.640] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004830)  
Thank you.

**[05:28:38.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004831)  
Yeah, there's a range.

**[05:28:39.840] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004832)  
I think now it's 3 to 3.5 million.

**[05:28:42.780] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004833)  
This was a figure agreed upon with DPWH.

**[05:28:45.840] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004834)  
Pero meron talagang mga lugar na malalayo.

**[05:28:49.060] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004835)  
Yung freight, maaring iba.

**[05:28:50.960] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004836)  
Tapos yung iba, baka iba rin yung fundasyon.

**[05:28:54.100] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004837)  
So, mas nagmamahal yun.

**[05:28:55.620] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004838)  
And yung binanggit niyo po, Cong Agyao, about the last mile school.

**[05:29:01.620] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004839)  
Pag-GEDA school.

**[05:29:02.860] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004840)  
Na walang kalsada.

**[05:29:03.660] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004841)  
Considered last mile school na rin ho siya.

**[05:29:05.660] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004842)  
So, mabibigyan priority siya doon under the LMS program of DepEd, Your Honor.

**[05:29:13.620] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004843)  
Thank you, Sec.

**[05:29:15.940] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004844)  
Madam Chair, sana po may include po doon sa Kalinga kasi lalo na sa mga Cordillera areas,

**[05:29:21.620] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004845)  
mga provinces, ang dami po naming lugar na hindi po talaga accessible.

**[05:29:25.780] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004846)  
But then, still, nandun po rin sila sa 2.5 million na regular fundasyon.

**[05:29:32.620] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004847)  
Funding po.

**[05:29:33.620] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004848)  
Madam Chair.

**[05:29:34.620] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004849)  
Yes, Your Honor, we'll definitely consider.

**[05:29:39.220] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004850)  
And, alam natin, 2.5 maka hindi na po sapat ngayon sa kasalukuyan, Your Honor.

**[05:29:45.180] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004851)  
Thank you, Madam Chair, and salamat sa ASEC.

**[05:29:48.240] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004852)  
Salamat po.

**[05:29:49.240] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-004853)  
Thank you, thank you to the DepEd family.

**[05:29:51.640] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-004854)  
Thank you very much.

**[05:29:53.420] Rep. Maria Carmen S. Zamora** (asr-004855)  
Next to ask a question is Representative Shirlyn Ba?as-Nograles.

**[05:29:59.770] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-004858)  
Magandang hapon po, Sec, and thank you, Madam Chair.

**[05:30:04.300] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-004865)  
Sec, may I just have two concerns po.

**[05:30:07.920] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-004866)  
First is still on the implementation of Republic Act 12080, or the Basic Education Mental Health Act, Sec.

**[05:30:17.880] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-004867)  
Because of the prevalence of mental health issues that happened among our schools, public schools,

**[05:30:25.980] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-004868)  
in fact, ngayon, we feel that many of our schools, hindi na po siya a safe learning space for our pupils and students.

**[05:30:37.360] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-004869)  
Just this morning, there was a stabbing incident in one of our public schools in General Santos City,

**[05:30:45.080] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-004870)  
among our 15-year-old high school students.

**[05:30:48.000] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-004871)  
So, SEC, isang nakita natin,

**[05:30:53.640] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-004872)  
yung pagtatala ng ating,

**[05:30:55.980] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-004873)  
guidance counsellors, diba?

**[05:30:58.580] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-004874)  
And it seems that there is a critical manpower shortage.

**[05:31:04.360] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-004875)  
We have 45,000, if my data is correct, we have 45,000 public schools.

**[05:31:10.080] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-004876)  
And we have less than, more or less, 1,000 licensed guidance counsellors.

**[05:31:16.480] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-004877)  
So, talagang napakalaki po ng disparity.

**[05:31:20.700] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-004878)  
And I understand na to address this,

**[05:31:24.800] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-004879)  
meron tayong mga associates,

**[05:31:25.980] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-004880)  
Tama po ba, SEC?

**[05:31:29.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004881)  
Tama po, Congresswoman, yung GCA and SCA, yung associates.

**[05:31:32.980] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-004882)

**[05:31:36.420] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-004883)

**[05:43:37.600] DepEd Assistant Secretary Marcelino G. Veloso III** (asr-005026)

**[05:43:41.300] DepEd Assistant Secretary Marcelino G. Veloso III** (asr-005027)

**[05:43:43.080] DepEd Assistant Secretary Marcelino G. Veloso III** (asr-005028)

**[05:43:55.240] DepEd Assistant Secretary Marcelino G. Veloso III** (asr-005032)

one of the tools that we've been exploring

**[05:44:00.280] DepEd Assistant Secretary Marcelino G. Veloso III** (asr-005034)

**[05:44:02.440] DepEd Assistant Secretary Marcelino G. Veloso III** (asr-005035)

**[05:44:07.040] DepEd Assistant Secretary Marcelino G. Veloso III** (asr-005036)

**[05:44:09.760] DepEd Assistant Secretary Marcelino G. Veloso III** (asr-005037)

**[05:44:12.080] DepEd Assistant Secretary Marcelino G. Veloso III** (asr-005038)

**[05:44:14.880] DepEd Assistant Secretary Marcelino G. Veloso III** (asr-005039)

**[05:44:17.400] DepEd Assistant Secretary Marcelino G. Veloso III** (asr-005040)

**[05:44:19.880] DepEd Assistant Secretary Marcelino G. Veloso III** (asr-005041)

**[05:55:17.940] Rep. Girlie E. Veloso** (asr-005167)

**[05:55:25.080] Rep. Girlie E. Veloso** (asr-005168)

**[05:55:44.280] Rep. Girlie E. Veloso** (asr-005171)  
values education not merely as a

**[05:55:47.940] Rep. Girlie E. Veloso** (asr-005172)  
classroom subjects but as values that our students can consistently understand

**[05:55:54.940] Rep. Girlie E. Veloso** (asr-005173)  
practice and carry with them inside and outside the school.

**[05:56:03.500] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005174)  
Salamat sa tanong, Your Honor, Madam Chair. Sa current implementation of Republic Act 11476 or the GMRC with

**[05:56:10.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005175)

**[05:56:17.940] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005176)  
meron po tayong tatlong phase ng training sa NAEAP or National

**[05:56:22.940] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005177)  
Educators Academy of the Philippines for grades 1 to 10. Development of lesson

**[05:56:28.280] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005178)  
exemplars for grades 1 to 3. So umpisahan ho natin nang maaga, habang maaga, ma'am, in

**[05:56:34.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005179)  
collaboration with the BCD or the Bureau of Curriculum Development. So

**[05:56:39.500] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005180)  
binabago na ho namin yung curriculum para mas ma-emphasize ho yung mga values, as you

**[05:56:45.200] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005181)  
mentioned, Your Honor, Madam Chair. Tapos yung

**[05:56:47.940] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005182)  
ongoing development ng mga lesson plans sa mga multigrade classes. So

**[05:56:52.100] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005183)  
kahit sila ay sama-sama, minsan may ganoon, grade

**[05:56:55.700] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005184)  
1, grade 2 magkasama sa isang class, mas natuturuan pa rin yung values, mas

**[05:57:00.100] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005185)  
nangingibabaw pa rin yung values, Your Honor.

**[05:57:03.860] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005186)  
Tapos itong ongoing development po ng

**[05:57:09.220] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005187)  
what's LAS?

**[05:57:13.300] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005188)  
Meron tayong mga learning activity sheets under the dynamic learning program. This is an

**[05:57:17.860] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005189)  
awarded teaching method sa Bohol

**[05:57:22.740] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005190)  
which Dr. Bernido won a Magsaysay Award for,

**[05:57:26.900] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005191)  
and it places an emphasis on independent learning and using the

**[05:57:33.060] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005192)

**[05:57:36.660] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005193)  
for dynamic learning na pioneered sa Bohol,

**[05:57:40.900] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005194)  
but it's also being used in other provinces like Sarangani

**[05:57:44.420] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005195)  
and even private schools use it, Your Honor.

**[05:57:47.860] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005196)

**[06:05:52.570] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005402)  
Aside from what tends to get a lot of airtime or publicity are the security measures, so

**[06:05:59.310] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005403)

**[06:06:05.710] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005404)

**[06:06:07.010] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005405)  
a fraction of the needed interventions, and as you mentioned the mental health interventions are

**[06:06:11.810] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005406)  
probably the more lasting ones and uh because if if a person is determined there's really

**[06:06:17.730] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005407)  
despite all of these physical uh obstacles there's still a way to to perform violent acts so

**[06:06:25.650] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005408)  
what we're trying to do is really build a larger sense of community so it's really

**[06:06:29.570] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005409)  
in a way it requires requires nothing short of a paradigm shift from our educators and uh even the

**[06:06:36.130] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005410)  
non non-teaching staff everyone who populates the school and the school community because

**[06:06:42.130] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005411)  
uh we're you know very simple interventions knowing the name of your learner

**[06:06:46.210] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005412)  
uh asking them or you know making sure they have someone to talk to in the course of the day

**[06:06:51.170] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005413)  
that no one is left alone and uh also ensuring that the children talk to each other we're also

**[06:06:56.370] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005414)  
considering uh you know uh limiting use of smartphones in schools unless it's for uh

**[06:07:06.130] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005415)  
educational purposes your honor so uh

**[06:07:12.290] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005416)  
but even we're even looking at the recess and the free time of the students because

**[06:07:17.170] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005417)  
we want them to socialize we want them to uh to to stay away from the gadgets where you know they

**[06:07:23.250] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005418)  
can be influenced by all sorts of uh things especially those who are 15 or 16 and below

**[06:07:28.610] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005419)  
so uh aside from this the we're also trying to ramp up the training of our personnel because not

**[06:07:36.130] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005420)  
everyone is trained how to deal with the administration of psychological first aid

**[06:07:42.290] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005421)  
Your Honor. So when Tacloban happened, we were really overwhelmed. There were

**[06:07:47.570] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005422)  
there were at least 400 people who needed immediate psychological first aid so things

**[06:07:52.770] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005423)
